   54 | function hasBrowserWindow(): boolean {
   55 |   return typeof globalThis.window !== "undefined";
   56 | }
   57 | 
   58 | function getBrowserWindow(): Window | null {
   59 |   return hasBrowserWindow() ? globalThis.window : null;
   60 | }
   61 | 
   62 | function prefersReducedMotion(): boolean {
   63 |   const browserWindow = getBrowserWindow();
   64 |   if (!browserWindow) return true;
   65 | 
   66 |   return (
   67 |     browserWindow.matchMedia?.("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)")?.matches ??
   68 |     false
   69 |   );
   70 | }
   71 | 
   72 | function useMediaQuery(query: string): boolean {
   73 |   const get = () => getBrowserWindow()?.matchMedia(query).matches ?? false;
   74 | 
   75 |   const [matches, setMatches] = React.useState<boolean>(get);
   76 | 
   77 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   78 |     const browserWindow = getBrowserWindow();
   79 |     if (!browserWindow) return;
   80 | 
   81 |     const mediaQueryList = browserWindow.matchMedia(query);
   82 |     const onChange = () => setMatches(mediaQueryList.matches);
   83 | 
   84 |     setMatches(mediaQueryList.matches);
   85 |     mediaQueryList.addEventListener("change", onChange);
   86 | 
   87 |     return () => mediaQueryList.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
   88 |   }, [query]);
   89 | 
   90 |   return matches;
   91 | }

  530 |           doc: isTipTapDoc(c.bodyRich) ? c.bodyRich : base.doc,
  531 |           err: "",
  532 |         },

  537 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  538 |     function targetNodeFromEvent(e: MouseEvent): Node | null {
  539 |       return e.target instanceof Node ? e.target : null;
  540 |     }
  541 | 
  542 |     function closeComposerIfEmpty(target: Node) {
  543 |       const composerEl = composerWrapRef.current;
  544 |       const clickedInComposer = Boolean(composerEl?.contains(target));
  545 |       const hasDraft = Boolean(draftRef.current.trim());
  546 | 
  547 |       if (!clickedInComposer && !hasDraft) {
  548 |         setComposerStage("collapsed");
  549 |       }
  550 |     }
  551 | 
  552 |     function closeEmptyReplies(target: Node) {
  553 |       for (const [commentId, draftState] of Object.entries(
  554 |         replyDraftsRef.current,
  555 |       )) {
  556 |         const wrapper = replyWrapByIdRef.current[commentId];
  557 |         const shouldClose =
  558 |           Boolean(draftState?.open) &&
  559 |           Boolean(wrapper) &&
  560 |           !wrapper?.contains(target) &&
  561 |           !draftState.plain.trim();
  562 | 
  563 |         if (shouldClose) deleteReplyDraft(commentId);
  564 |       }
  565 |     }
  566 | 
  567 |     function closeEmptyEdits(target: Node) {
  568 |       for (const [commentId, draftState] of Object.entries(
  569 |         editDraftsRef.current,
  570 |       )) {
  571 |         const wrapper = editWrapByIdRef.current[commentId];
  572 |         const shouldClose =
  573 |           Boolean(draftState?.open) &&
  574 |           Boolean(wrapper) &&
  575 |           !wrapper?.contains(target) &&
  576 |           !draftState.plain.trim();
  577 | 
  578 |         if (shouldClose) deleteEditDraft(commentId);
  579 |       }
  580 |     }
  581 | 
  582 |     function closeAutoClosableReports(target: Node) {
  583 |       for (const [commentId, draftState] of Object.entries(
  584 |         reportDraftsRef.current,
  585 |       )) {
  586 |         const wrapper = reportWrapByIdRef.current[commentId];
  587 |         const canAutoClose =
  588 |           Boolean(draftState.done) || !draftState.reason.trim();
  589 |         const shouldClose =
  590 |           draftState.open &&
  591 |           Boolean(wrapper) &&
  592 |           !wrapper?.contains(target) &&
  593 |           canAutoClose;
  594 | 
  595 |         if (shouldClose) deleteReportDraft(commentId);
  596 |       }
  597 |     }
  598 | 
  599 |     function onMouseDown(e: MouseEvent) {
  600 |       const target = targetNodeFromEvent(e);
  601 |       if (!target) return;
  602 | 
  603 |       closeComposerIfEmpty(target);
  604 |       closeEmptyReplies(target);
  605 |       closeEmptyEdits(target);
  606 |       closeAutoClosableReports(target);
  607 |     }
  608 | 
  609 |     globalThis.document.addEventListener("mousedown", onMouseDown, true);
  610 |     return () =>
  611 |       globalThis.document.removeEventListener("mousedown", onMouseDown, true);
  612 |   }, []);

  174 | function CommentHeader(props: CommentHeaderProps) {

    5 | export function reorderRootsPinnedFirst(
    6 |   roots: Array<{ rootId: string; comments: CommentDTO[] }>,
    7 |   pinnedCommentId: string | null,
    8 | ) {
    9 |   const pid = (pinnedCommentId ?? "").trim();
   10 |   if (!pid) return roots;
   11 | 
   12 |   const idx = roots.findIndex((r) =>
   13 |     (r.comments ?? []).some((c) => c.id === pid),
   14 |   );
   15 |   if (idx <= 0) return roots;
   16 | 
   17 |   const pinned = roots[idx];
   18 |   const rest = roots.slice(0, idx).concat(roots.slice(idx + 1));
   19 |   return [pinned, ...rest];
   20 | }
   21 | 
   22 | export function parseHash(): {
   23 |   lineKey?: string;
   24 |   commentId?: string;
   25 |   rootId?: string;
   26 | } {
   27 |   if (typeof window === "undefined") return {};
   28 |   const raw = (window.location.hash ?? "").replace(/^#/, "").trim();
   29 |   if (!raw) return {};
   30 |   const sp = new URLSearchParams(raw);
   31 |   const lineKey = (sp.get("l") ?? "").trim();
   32 |   const commentId = (sp.get("c") ?? "").trim();
   33 |   const rootId = (sp.get("root") ?? "").trim();
   34 |   return {
   35 |     lineKey: lineKey || undefined,
   36 |     commentId: commentId || undefined,
   37 |     rootId: rootId || undefined,
   38 |   };
   39 | }

   41 | export function cueGroupKey(lyrics: LyricsApiOk, lineKey: string): string {
   42 |   const lk = (lineKey ?? "").trim();
   43 |   if (!lk) return "";
   44 |   const mapped = lyrics.groupMap?.[lk]?.canonicalGroupKey;
   45 |   if (typeof mapped === "string" && mapped.trim()) return mapped.trim();
   46 |   const cue = (lyrics.cues ?? []).find((x) => x.lineKey === lk);
   47 |   const groupKey = cue?.canonicalGroupKey;
   48 |   return typeof groupKey === "string" ? groupKey.trim() : "";
   49 | }
   50 | 
   51 | export function isSameGroup(a: string, b: string): boolean {
   52 |   const aa = (a ?? "").trim();
   53 |   const bb = (b ?? "").trim();
   54 |   return !!aa && !!bb && aa === bb;
   55 | }

   98 | export function rootRecentTs(root: {
   99 |   rootId: string;
  100 |   comments: CommentDTO[];

  103 |   for (const comment of root.comments ?? []) {
  104 |     const timestamp = Date.parse((comment.editedAt ?? comment.createdAt) as string);
  105 |     if (!Number.isNaN(timestamp)) best = Math.max(best, timestamp);

  110 | export function formatAgo(iso: string | null | undefined): string {
  111 |   const timestamp = Date.parse((iso ?? "") as string);
  112 |   if (!Number.isFinite(timestamp)) return "";
  113 | 
  114 |   const now = Date.now();
  115 |   const seconds = Math.max(0, Math.floor((now - timestamp) / 1000));
  116 | 
  117 |   if (seconds < 60) return "just now";
  118 |   const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
  119 |   if (minutes < 60) return `${minutes}m ago`;
  120 |   const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
  121 |   if (hours < 24) return `${hours}h ago`;
  122 |   const days = Math.floor(hours / 24);
  123 |   if (days < 7) return `${days}d ago`;
  124 |   const weeks = Math.floor(days / 7);
  125 |   if (weeks < 52) return `${weeks}w ago`;
  126 |   const years = Math.floor(days / 365);
  127 |   return `${Math.max(1, years)}y ago`;
  128 | }

   94 |         ""
   95 |       ).trim();
   96 |       const commentId = (detail?.commentId ?? h.commentId ?? "").trim();
   97 |       const rootId = (detail?.rootId ?? h.rootId ?? "").trim();
   98 | 
   99 |       setFocusedRootId(rootId);
  100 | 
  101 |       if (!lineKey) {
  102 |         setSelected(null);
  103 |         return;
  104 |       }
  105 | 
  106 |       const pick = cues.find((c) => c.lineKey === lineKey);
  107 |       if (!pick) {
  108 |         setSelected(null);
  109 |         return;
  110 |       }
  111 | 
  112 |       if (pendingLineKey === lineKey) {
  113 |         clearPendingLineKey();
  114 |       }
  115 | 
  116 |       setSelected({
  117 |         lineKey: pick.lineKey,
  118 |         lineText: pick.text,
  119 |         tMs: pick.tMs,
  120 |         groupKey:
  121 |           (groupMap?.[pick.lineKey]?.canonicalGroupKey ??
  122 |             pick.canonicalGroupKey ??
  123 |             "") ||
  124 |           undefined,
  125 |       });
  126 | 
  127 |       pendingScrollCommentIdRef.current = commentId;
  128 |       openFromHashRef.current = true;
  129 |     }
  130 | 
  131 |     function onNativeHashNavigation() {
  132 |       applyHashState();
  133 |     }
  134 | 
  135 |     function onAppHashNavigation(event: Event) {
  136 |       const detail =
  137 |         event instanceof CustomEvent && isHashNavigationDetail(event.detail)
  138 |           ? event.detail
  139 |           : undefined;
  140 | 
  141 |       applyHashState(detail);
  142 |     }
  143 | 
  144 |     applyHashState();
  145 | 
  146 |     globalThis.window.addEventListener("hashchange", onNativeHashNavigation);
  147 |     globalThis.window.addEventListener("popstate", onNativeHashNavigation);
  148 |     globalThis.window.addEventListener(
  149 |       EXEGESIS_HASH_NAV_EVENT,
  150 |       onAppHashNavigation,
  151 |     );
  152 | 
  153 |     return () => {
  154 |       globalThis.window.removeEventListener(
  155 |         "hashchange",
  156 |         onNativeHashNavigation,
  157 |       );
  158 |       globalThis.window.removeEventListener("popstate", onNativeHashNavigation);
  159 |       globalThis.window.removeEventListener(
  160 |         EXEGESIS_HASH_NAV_EVENT,
  161 |         onAppHashNavigation,
  162 |       );
  163 |     };
  164 |   }, [lyricsRecordingId, cues, groupMap, setSelected, setFocusedRootId]);
  165 | 
  166 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  167 |     if (!isMobile) return;
  168 |     if (!selectedLineKey) return;
  169 |     if (!openFromHashRef.current) return;
  170 | 
  171 |     setDrawerOpen(true);
  172 |     openFromHashRef.current = false;
  173 |   }, [isMobile, selectedLineKey, setDrawerOpen]);
  174 | 
  175 |   return {
  176 |     pendingScrollCommentIdRef,
  177 |   };
  178 | }

  288 |   async function submitReport(commentId: string) {
  289 |     if (!canReport) return;
  290 | 
  291 |     const draft0 = reportByCommentId[commentId];
  292 |     if (!draft0) return;
  293 | 
  294 |     const category = (draft0.category ?? "").trim();
  295 |     const reason = (draft0.reason ?? "").trim();
  296 | 
  297 |     setReportByCommentId((prev) => ({
  298 |       ...prev,
  299 |       [commentId]: { ...draft0, busy: true, err: "" },
  300 |     }));
  301 | 
  302 |     try {
  303 |       const r = await fetch("/api/exegesis/report", {
  304 |         method: "POST",
  305 |         headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
  306 |         body: JSON.stringify({ commentId, category, reason }),
  307 |       });
  308 | 
  309 |       const j = (await r.json()) as ReportOk | ReportErr;
  310 | 
  311 |       if (!j.ok) {
  312 |         setReportByCommentId((prev) => ({
  313 |           ...prev,
  314 |           [commentId]: {
  315 |             ...draft0,
  316 |             busy: false,
  317 |             err: j.error || "Report failed.",
  318 |           },
  319 |         }));
  320 |         return;
  321 |       }
  322 | 
  323 |       setReportByCommentId((prev) => ({
  324 |         ...prev,
  325 |         [commentId]: { ...draft0, busy: false, done: true, err: "" },
  326 |       }));
  327 |     } catch {
  328 |       setReportByCommentId((prev) => ({
  329 |         ...prev,
  330 |         [commentId]: { ...draft0, busy: false, err: "Report failed." },
  331 |       }));
  332 |     }
  333 |   }

  382 |   async function submitEdit(comment: CommentDTO) {
  383 |     if (!canPost) {
  384 |       setThreadErr("Patron or Partner required to edit.");
  385 |       return;
  386 |     }
  387 |     if (isLocked) {
  388 |       setThreadErr("Thread is locked.");
  389 |       return;
  390 |     }
  391 |     if (!thread) {
  392 |       setThreadErr("Thread not loaded yet.");
  393 |       return;
  394 |     }
  395 |     if (!viewerMemberId || comment.createdByMemberId !== viewerMemberId) {
  396 |       setThreadErr("You can only edit your own comments.");
  397 |       return;
  398 |     }
  399 |     if (comment.status !== "live") {
  400 |       setThreadErr("Only live comments can be edited.");
  401 |       return;
  402 |     }
  403 | 
  404 |     const draft0 = editByCommentId[comment.id];
  405 |     if (!draft0 || !draft0.open) return;
  406 | 
  407 |     const text = (draft0.plain ?? "").trim();
  408 |     if (!text) {
  409 |       setEditByCommentId((prev) => ({
  410 |         ...prev,
  411 |         [comment.id]: { ...draft0, err: "Edit is empty." },
  412 |       }));
  413 |       return;
  414 |     }
  415 | 
  416 |     setEditByCommentId((prev) => ({
  417 |       ...prev,
  418 |       [comment.id]: { ...draft0, posting: true, err: "" },
  419 |     }));
  420 |     setThreadErr("");
  421 | 
  422 |     const doc =
  423 |       draft0.doc ??
  424 |       ({
  425 |         type: "doc",
  426 |         content: [{ type: "paragraph", content: [{ type: "text", text }] }],
  427 |       } as const);
  428 | 
  429 |     try {
  430 |       const r = await fetch("/api/exegesis/comment/edit", {
  431 |         method: "POST",
  432 |         headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
  433 |         body: JSON.stringify({
  434 |           commentId: comment.id,
  435 |           bodyPlain: text,
  436 |           bodyRich: doc,
  437 |         }),
  438 |       });
  439 | 
  440 |       const j = (await r.json()) as CommentEditOk | CommentEditErr;
  441 | 
  442 |       if (!j.ok) {
  443 |         setEditByCommentId((prev) => ({
  444 |           ...prev,
  445 |           [comment.id]: {
  446 |             ...draft0,
  447 |             posting: false,
  448 |             err: j.error || "Edit failed.",
  449 |           },
  450 |         }));
  451 |         return;
  452 |       }
  453 | 
  454 |       setThread((prev) => {
  455 |         if (!prev) return prev;
  456 | 
  457 |         const roots = (prev.roots ?? []).map((root) => ({
  458 |           ...root,
  459 |           comments: (root.comments ?? []).map((x) =>
  460 |             x.id === j.comment.id ? { ...x, ...j.comment } : x,
  461 |           ),
  462 |         }));
  463 | 
  464 |         return { ...prev, roots, meta: j.meta };
  465 |       });
  466 | 
  467 |       closeEdit(comment.id);
  468 |     } finally {
  469 |       setEditByCommentId((prev) => {
  470 |         const cur = prev[comment.id];
  471 |         if (!cur) return prev;
  472 |         if (!cur.posting) return prev;
  473 |         return { ...prev, [comment.id]: { ...cur, posting: false } };
  474 |       });
  475 |     }
  476 |   }
  477 | 
  478 |   async function postComment() {
  479 |     if (!selected) return;
  480 |     if (!canPost) {
  481 |       setThreadErr("Patron or Partner required to post.");
  482 |       return;
  483 |     }
  484 | 
  485 |     if (thread?.meta?.locked) {
  486 |       setThreadErr("Thread is locked.");
  487 |       return;
  488 |     }
  489 | 
  490 |     const groupKey = (thread?.groupKey ?? "").trim();
  491 |     if (!groupKey) {
  492 |       setThreadErr("Thread not loaded yet.");
  493 |       return;
  494 |     }
  495 | 
  496 |     const text = draft.trim();
  497 |     if (!text) return;
  498 | 
  499 |     setPosting(true);
  500 |     setThreadErr("");
  501 | 
  502 |     try {
  503 |       const doc =
  504 |         draftDoc ??
  505 |         ({
  506 |           type: "doc",
  507 |           content: [
  508 |             {
  509 |               type: "paragraph",
  510 |               content: [{ type: "text", text }],
  511 |             },
  512 |           ],
  513 |         } as const);
  514 | 
  515 |       const r = await fetch("/api/exegesis/comment", {
  516 |         method: "POST",
  517 |         headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
  518 |         body: JSON.stringify({
  519 |           recordingId,
  520 |           lineKey: selected.lineKey,
  521 |           groupKey: (thread?.groupKey ?? "").trim(),
  522 |           parentId: null,
  523 |           bodyPlain: text,
  524 |           bodyRich: doc,
  525 |           tMs: selected.tMs,
  526 |           lineTextSnapshot: selected.lineText,
  527 |           lyricsVersion: lyricsVersion ?? null,
  528 |         }),
  529 |       });
  530 | 
  531 |       const j = (await r.json()) as
  532 |         | (CommentPostOk & { newlyAwardedBadges?: unknown })
  533 |         | {
  534 |             ok: false;
  535 |             error: string;
  536 |             gate?: ThreadApiErr["gate"];
  537 |             newlyAwardedBadges?: unknown;
  538 |           };
  539 | 
  540 |       if (!j.ok) {
  541 |         if (j.gate) {
  542 |           const res = gateResultFromPayload({
  543 |             payload: j.gate,
  544 |             attempt: { verb: "postComment", domain: "exegesis" },
  545 |             isSignedIn: Boolean(userId),
  546 |             intent: "explicit",
  547 |           });
  548 | 
  549 |           if (!res.ok) {
  550 |             applyGateResult(res);
  551 |             setThreadErr("");
  552 |             return;
  553 |           }
  554 |         }
  555 | 
  556 |         setThreadErr(j.error || "Failed to post comment.");
  557 |         return;
  558 |       }
  559 | 
  560 |       setDraft("");
  561 |       setDraftDoc(null);
  562 |       announceAwardPayload(j.newlyAwardedBadges);
  563 | 
  564 |       pendingScrollCommentIdRef.current = j.comment.id;
  565 |       setHash({
  566 |         lineKey: selected.lineKey,
  567 |         commentId: j.comment.id,
  568 |         rootId: focusedRootId || undefined,
  569 |       });
  570 | 
  571 |       setThread((prev) => {
  572 |         if (!prev) return prev;
  573 |         if (
  574 |           prev.recordingId !== j.recordingId ||
  575 |           prev.groupKey !== j.groupKey
  576 |         ) {
  577 |           return prev;
  578 |         }
  579 | 
  580 |         const newRoot = { rootId: j.comment.rootId, comments: [j.comment] };
  581 |         return {
  582 |           ...prev,
  583 |           meta: j.meta,
  584 |           roots: [newRoot, ...prev.roots],
  585 |           identities: { ...prev.identities, ...j.identities },
  586 |         };
  587 |       });
  588 | 
  589 |       const url =
  590 |         `/api/exegesis/thread?recordingId=${encodeURIComponent(recordingId)}` +
  591 |         `&groupKey=${encodeURIComponent(groupKey)}`;
  592 | 
  593 |       fetch(url, { cache: "no-store" })
  594 |         .then((r2) => r2.json())
  595 |         .then((jj: ThreadApiOk | ThreadApiErr) => {
  596 |           if (jj && (jj as ThreadApiOk).ok) {
  597 |             setThread(jj as ThreadApiOk);
  598 |             setThreadErr("");
  599 |           }
  600 |         })
  601 |         .catch(() => {});
  602 |     } finally {
  603 |       setPosting(false);
  604 |     }
  605 |   }
  606 | 
  607 |   async function postReply(parentComment: CommentDTO) {
  608 |     if (!selected) return;
  609 |     if (!canPost) {
  610 |       setThreadErr("Patron or Partner required to post.");
  611 |       return;
  612 |     }
  613 |     if (isLocked) {
  614 |       setThreadErr("Thread is locked.");
  615 |       return;
  616 |     }
  617 |     if (!thread) {
  618 |       setThreadErr("Thread not loaded yet.");
  619 |       return;
  620 |     }
  621 | 
  622 |     const parentId = parentComment.id;
  623 |     const draft0 = replyByCommentId[parentId];
  624 |     if (!draft0 || !draft0.open) return;
  625 | 
  626 |     const text = (draft0.plain ?? "").trim();
  627 |     if (!text) {
  628 |       setReplyByCommentId((prev) => ({
  629 |         ...prev,
  630 |         [parentId]: { ...draft0, err: "Reply is empty." },
  631 |       }));
  632 |       return;
  633 |     }
  634 | 
  635 |     if ((parentComment.depth ?? 0) + 1 > 6) {
  636 |       setReplyByCommentId((prev) => ({
  637 |         ...prev,
  638 |         [parentId]: { ...draft0, err: "Thread depth limit reached." },
  639 |       }));
  640 |       return;
  641 |     }
  642 | 
  643 |     setReplyByCommentId((prev) => ({
  644 |       ...prev,
  645 |       [parentId]: { ...draft0, posting: true, err: "" },
  646 |     }));
  647 |     setThreadErr("");
  648 | 
  649 |     const groupKey = (thread.groupKey ?? "").trim();
  650 | 
  651 |     try {
  652 |       const doc =
  653 |         draft0.doc ??
  654 |         ({
  655 |           type: "doc",
  656 |           content: [
  657 |             {
  658 |               type: "paragraph",
  659 |               content: [{ type: "text", text }],
  660 |             },
  661 |           ],
  662 |         } as const);
  663 | 
  664 |       const r = await fetch("/api/exegesis/comment", {
  665 |         method: "POST",
  666 |         headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
  667 |         body: JSON.stringify({
  668 |           recordingId,
  669 |           lineKey: selected.lineKey,
  670 |           groupKey,
  671 |           parentId,
  672 |           bodyPlain: text,
  673 |           bodyRich: doc,
  674 |           tMs: selected.tMs,
  675 |           lineTextSnapshot: selected.lineText,
  676 |           lyricsVersion: lyricsVersion ?? null,
  677 |         }),
  678 |       });
  679 | 
  680 |       const j = (await r.json()) as
  681 |         | (CommentPostOk & { newlyAwardedBadges?: unknown })
  682 |         | {
  683 |             ok: false;
  684 |             error: string;
  685 |             gate?: ThreadApiErr["gate"];
  686 |             newlyAwardedBadges?: unknown;
  687 |           };
  688 | 
  689 |       if (!j.ok) {
  690 |         if (j.gate) {
  691 |           const res = gateResultFromPayload({
  692 |             payload: j.gate,
  693 |             attempt: { verb: "postComment", domain: "exegesis" },
  694 |             isSignedIn: Boolean(userId),
  695 |             intent: "explicit",
  696 |           });
  697 | 
  698 |           if (!res.ok) {
  699 |             applyGateResult(res);
  700 |             setThreadErr("");
  701 |             return;
  702 |           }
  703 |         }
  704 | 
  705 |         setThreadErr(j.error || "Reply failed.");
  706 |         return;
  707 |       }
  708 | 
  709 |       setReplyByCommentId((prev) => ({
  710 |         ...prev,
  711 |         [parentId]: {
  712 |           ...draft0,
  713 |           posting: false,
  714 |           open: false,
  715 |           plain: "",
  716 |           doc: null,
  717 |           err: "",
  718 |         },
  719 |       }));
  720 |       announceAwardPayload(j.newlyAwardedBadges);
  721 | 
  722 |       pendingScrollCommentIdRef.current = j.comment.id;
  723 |       setHash({
  724 |         lineKey: selected.lineKey,
  725 |         commentId: j.comment.id,
  726 |         rootId: focusedRootId || undefined,
  727 |       });
  728 | 
  729 |       setThread((prev) => {
  730 |         if (!prev) return prev;
  731 |         if (
  732 |           prev.recordingId !== j.recordingId ||
  733 |           prev.groupKey !== j.groupKey
  734 |         ) {
  735 |           return prev;
  736 |         }
  737 | 
  738 |         const roots = (prev.roots ?? []).map((root) => {
  739 |           if (root.rootId !== j.comment.rootId) return root;
  740 |           return { ...root, comments: [...(root.comments ?? []), j.comment] };
  741 |         });
  742 | 
  743 |         const found = roots.some((r0) => r0.rootId === j.comment.rootId);
  744 |         const roots2 = found
  745 |           ? roots
  746 |           : roots.concat([{ rootId: j.comment.rootId, comments: [j.comment] }]);
  747 | 
  748 |         return {
  749 |           ...prev,
  750 |           meta: j.meta,
  751 |           roots: roots2,
  752 |           identities: { ...prev.identities, ...j.identities },
  753 |         };
  754 |       });
  755 | 
  756 |       const url =
  757 |         `/api/exegesis/thread?recordingId=${encodeURIComponent(recordingId)}` +
  758 |         `&groupKey=${encodeURIComponent(groupKey)}`;
  759 | 
  760 |       fetch(url, { cache: "no-store" })
  761 |         .then((r2) => r2.json())
  762 |         .then((jj: ThreadApiOk | ThreadApiErr) => {
  763 |           if (jj && (jj as ThreadApiOk).ok) {
  764 |             setThread(jj as ThreadApiOk);
  765 |             setThreadErr("");
  766 |           }
  767 |         })
  768 |         .catch(() => {});
  769 |     } finally {
  770 |       setReplyByCommentId((prev) => {
  771 |         const cur = prev[parentId];
  772 |         if (!cur) return prev;
  773 |         if (!cur.posting) return prev;
  774 |         return { ...prev, [parentId]: { ...cur, posting: false } };
  775 |       });
  776 |     }
  777 |   }
  778 | 
  779 |   async function toggleVote(commentId: string) {
  780 |     if (!thread) return;
  781 | 
  782 |     if (!canVote) {
  783 |       setThreadErr(
  784 |         thread.viewer.kind === "anon"
  785 |           ? "Sign in to vote."
  786 |           : "Friend tier or higher required to vote.",
  787 |       );
  788 |       return;
  789 |     }
  790 | 
  791 |     setThreadErr("");
  792 | 
  793 |     setThread((prev) => {
  794 |       if (!prev) return prev;
  795 |       const roots = prev.roots.map((r) => ({
  796 |         ...r,
  797 |         comments: r.comments.map((c) => {
  798 |           if (c.id !== commentId) return c;
  799 |           const nextHas = !c.viewerHasVoted;
  800 |           const nextCount = Math.max(0, c.voteCount + (nextHas ? 1 : -1));
  801 |           return { ...c, viewerHasVoted: nextHas, voteCount: nextCount };
  802 |         }),
  803 |       }));
  804 |       return { ...prev, roots };
  805 |     });
  806 | 
  807 |     const r = await fetch("/api/exegesis/vote", {
  808 |       method: "POST",
  809 |       headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
  810 |       body: JSON.stringify({ commentId }),
  811 |     });
  812 | 
  813 |     const j = (await r.json()) as
  814 |       | (VoteOk & { newlyAwardedBadges?: unknown })
  815 |       | (VoteErr & { newlyAwardedBadges?: unknown });
  816 | 
  817 |     if (!j.ok) {
  818 |       if (j.gate) {
  819 |         const res = gateResultFromPayload({
  820 |           payload: j.gate,
  821 |           attempt: { verb: "vote", domain: "exegesis" },
  822 |           isSignedIn: Boolean(userId),
  823 |           intent: "explicit",
  824 |         });
  825 | 
  826 |         if (!res.ok) {
  827 |           applyGateResult(res);
  828 |           setThreadErr("");
  829 |           return;
  830 |         }
  831 |       }
  832 | 
  833 |       setThreadErr(j.error || "Vote failed.");
  834 | 
  835 |       if (selected) {
  836 |         const gk = (thread?.groupKey ?? "").trim();
  837 |         const url =
  838 |           `/api/exegesis/thread?recordingId=${encodeURIComponent(recordingId)}` +
  839 |           (gk
  840 |             ? `&groupKey=${encodeURIComponent(gk)}`
  841 |             : `&lineKey=${encodeURIComponent(selected.lineKey)}`);
  842 |         const rr = await fetch(url, { cache: "no-store" });
  843 |         const jj = (await rr.json()) as ThreadApiOk | ThreadApiErr;
  844 |         if (jj.ok) {
  845 |           setThread(jj);
  846 |           setThreadErr("");
  847 |         }
  848 |       }
  849 |       return;
  850 |     }
  851 | 
  852 |     setThread((prev) => {
  853 |       if (!prev) return prev;
  854 |       const roots = prev.roots.map((r0) => ({
  855 |         ...r0,
  856 |         comments: r0.comments.map((c) =>
  857 |           c.id === j.commentId
  858 |             ? { ...c, viewerHasVoted: j.viewerHasVoted, voteCount: j.voteCount }
  859 |             : c,
  860 |         ),
  861 |       }));
  862 |       return { ...prev, roots };
  863 |     });
  864 | 
  865 |     announceAwardPayload(j.newlyAwardedBadges);
  866 |   }
  867 | 
  868 |   return {
  869 |     thread,
  870 |     setThread,
  871 |     threadErr,
  872 |     setThreadErr,
  873 |     threadUI,
  874 |     shouldShowInitialShimmer,
  875 |     viewerMemberId,
  876 |     viewerIdentity,
  877 |     meta,
  878 |     isLocked,
  879 |     canVote,
  880 |     canReport,
  881 |     canPost,
  882 |     canClaimName,
  883 |     rootsForRender,
  884 |     threadKey,
  885 |     isAnon,
  886 |     submitReport,
  887 |     submitClaimName,
  888 |     submitEdit,
  889 |     postComment,
  890 |     postReply,
  891 |     toggleVote,
  892 |   };
  893 | }

   61 | async function requireCanPost(memberId: string): Promise<boolean> {
   62 |   return await hasAnyEntitlement(memberId, [

   68 | export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
   69 |   const correlationId = correlationIdFromRequest(req);
   70 | 
   71 |   let raw: unknown;
   72 |   try {
   73 |     raw = await req.json();
   74 |   } catch {
   75 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
   76 |       ok: false,
   77 |       error: "Invalid JSON body.",
   78 |     });
   79 |   }
   80 | 
   81 |   const b = bodyRecord(raw);
   82 |   if (!b) {
   83 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
   84 |       ok: false,
   85 |       error: "Invalid JSON body.",
   86 |     });
   87 |   }
   88 | 
   89 |   const commentId = normString(b.commentId);
   90 |   if (!commentId || !isUuid(commentId)) {
   91 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
   92 |       ok: false,
   93 |       error: "Invalid commentId.",
   94 |     });
   95 |   }
   96 | 
   97 |   if (!("bodyRich" in b)) {
   98 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
   99 |       ok: false,
  100 |       error: "Missing bodyRich.",
  101 |     });
  102 |   }
  103 |   const bodyRichInput: unknown = b.bodyRich ?? null;
  104 | 
  105 |   const v = validateAndSanitizeTipTapDoc(bodyRichInput);
  106 |   if (!v.ok) {
  107 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, { ok: false, error: v.error });
  108 |   }
  109 | 
  110 |   const bodyPlain = v.plain;
  111 | 
  112 |   let bodyRichJson = "";
  113 |   try {
  114 |     bodyRichJson = JSON.stringify(v.doc);
  115 |   } catch {
  116 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  117 |       ok: false,
  118 |       error: "Invalid bodyRich.",
  119 |     });
  120 |   }
  121 | 
  122 |   if (bodyRichJson.length > 200_000) {
  123 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  124 |       ok: false,
  125 |       error: "bodyRich too large.",
  126 |     });
  127 |   }
  128 | 
  129 |   const memberId = await requireExegesisMemberId();
  130 |   if (!memberId) {
  131 |     return gateError(req, {
  132 |       correlationId,
  133 |       status: 401,
  134 |       domain: "exegesis",
  135 |       code: "AUTH_REQUIRED",
  136 |       action: "login",
  137 |       message: "Sign in to edit a comment.",
  138 |     });
  139 |   }
  140 | 
  141 |   if (!isUuid(memberId)) {
  142 |     return gateError(req, {
  143 |       correlationId,
  144 |       status: 403,
  145 |       domain: "exegesis",
  146 |       code: "PROVISIONING",
  147 |       action: "wait",
  148 |       message: "Provisioning required.",
  149 |     });
  150 |   }
  151 | 
  152 |   const canPost = await requireCanPost(memberId);
  153 |   if (!canPost) {
  154 |     return gateError(req, {
  155 |       correlationId,
  156 |       status: 403,
  157 |       domain: "exegesis",
  158 |       code: "TIER_REQUIRED",
  159 |       action: "subscribe",
  160 |       message: "Editing requires Patron or Partner.",
  161 |     });
  162 |   }
  163 | 
  164 |   try {
  165 |     const q = await sql<{
  166 |       guard_err: string | null;
  167 |       id: string | null;
  168 |       track_id: string | null;
  169 |       group_key: string | null;
  170 |       line_key: string | null;
  171 |       parent_id: string | null;
  172 |       root_id: string | null;
  173 |       depth: number | null;
  174 |       body_rich: unknown | null;
  175 |       body_plain: string | null;
  176 |       t_ms: number | null;
  177 |       line_text_snapshot: string | null;
  178 |       lyrics_version: string | null;
  179 |       created_by_member_id: string | null;
  180 |       status: "live" | "hidden" | "deleted" | null;
  181 |       created_at: string | null;
  182 |       edited_at: string | null;
  183 |       edit_count: number | null;
  184 |       vote_count: number | null;
  185 |       meta_track_id: string | null;
  186 |       meta_group_key: string | null;
  187 |       meta_pinned_comment_id: string | null;
  188 |       meta_locked: boolean | null;
  189 |       meta_comment_count: number | null;
  190 |       meta_last_activity_at: string | null;
  191 |       meta_created_at: string | null;
  192 |       meta_updated_at: string | null;
  193 |     }>`
  194 |     with
  195 | one as (select 1 as one),
  196 | params as (
  197 |   select
  198 |     ${commentId}::uuid     as comment_id,
  199 |     ${memberId}::uuid      as member_id,

  245 |   from one

  247 |   left join meta_pre mp on true
  248 |   left join params p on true

  269 |   set

  273 |     and m.group_key = (select group_key from target)

  275 |   returning m.track_id, m.group_key, m.pinned_comment_id, m.locked,

  278 | meta_out as (
  279 |   select * from meta_upd
  280 |   union all
  281 |   select * from meta_pre

  283 | )
  284 | select
  285 |   g.err as guard_err,
  286 |   u.id,
  287 |   u.track_id,
  288 |   u.group_key,
  289 |   u.line_key,
  290 |   u.parent_id,
  291 |   u.root_id,
  292 |   u.depth,
  293 |   u.body_rich,
  294 |   u.body_plain,
  295 |   u.t_ms,
  296 |   u.line_text_snapshot,

  305 |   m.group_key as meta_group_key,
  306 |   m.pinned_comment_id as meta_pinned_comment_id,

  312 | from one

  317 |     `;

  320 |     if (!row) {

  322 |         ok: false,

  332 |         });
  333 |       }
  334 |       if (row.guard_err === "LOCKED") {
  335 |         return gateError(req, {
  336 |           correlationId,
  337 |           status: 403,
  338 |           domain: "exegesis",
  339 |           code: "INVALID_REQUEST",
  340 |           action: "wait",
  341 |           message: "Thread is locked.",
  342 |         });
  343 |       }
  344 |       if (row.guard_err === "FORBIDDEN") {
  345 |         return gateError(req, {
  346 |           correlationId,
  347 |           status: 403,
  348 |           domain: "exegesis",
  349 |           code: "INVALID_REQUEST",
  350 |           action: "wait",
  351 |           message: "You can only edit your own comments.",
  352 |         });
  353 |       }
  354 |       if (row.guard_err === "DELETED") {
  355 |         return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  356 |           ok: false,
  357 |           error: "Cannot edit a deleted comment.",
  358 |         });
  359 |       }
  360 |       if (row.guard_err === "HIDDEN") {
  361 |         return gateError(req, {
  362 |           correlationId,
  363 |           status: 403,
  364 |           domain: "exegesis",
  365 |           code: "INVALID_REQUEST",
  366 |           action: "wait",
  367 |           message: "Cannot edit a hidden comment.",
  368 |         });
  369 |       }
  370 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  371 |         ok: false,
  372 |         error: "Cannot edit comment.",
  373 |       });
  374 |     }
  375 | 
  376 |     if (!row.id || !row.track_id || !row.group_key) {
  377 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 500, {
  378 |         ok: false,
  379 |         error: "Edit failed.",
  380 |       });
  381 |     }
  382 | 
  383 |     const comment: CommentDTO = {
  384 |       id: row.id,
  385 |       recordingId: row.track_id,
  386 |       groupKey: row.group_key,
  387 |       lineKey: row.line_key ?? "",
  388 |       parentId: row.parent_id ?? null,
  389 |       rootId: row.root_id ?? row.id,
  390 |       depth: Number(row.depth ?? 0),
  391 |       bodyRich: row.body_rich ?? {},
  392 |       bodyPlain: row.body_plain ?? "",
  393 |       tMs: row.t_ms ?? null,
  394 |       lineTextSnapshot: row.line_text_snapshot ?? "",
  395 |       lyricsVersion: row.lyrics_version ?? null,
  396 |       createdByMemberId: row.created_by_member_id ?? "",
  397 |       status: (row.status ?? "live") as "live" | "hidden" | "deleted",
  398 |       createdAt: row.created_at ?? "",
  399 |       editedAt: row.edited_at ?? null,
  400 |       editCount: Number(row.edit_count ?? 0),
  401 |       voteCount: Number(row.vote_count ?? 0),
  402 |       viewerHasVoted: false,
  403 |     };
  404 | 
  405 |     const meta: ThreadMetaDTO = {
  406 |       recordingId: String(row.meta_track_id ?? row.track_id),
  407 |       groupKey: String(row.meta_group_key ?? row.group_key),
  408 |       pinnedCommentId: row.meta_pinned_comment_id ?? null,
  409 |       locked: Boolean(row.meta_locked),
  410 |       commentCount: Number(row.meta_comment_count ?? 0),
  411 |       lastActivityAt: String(row.meta_last_activity_at ?? ""),
  412 |       createdAt: String(row.meta_created_at ?? ""),
  413 |       updatedAt: String(row.meta_updated_at ?? ""),
  414 |     };
  415 | 
  416 |     return jsonOk<ApiOk>({ ok: true, comment, meta }, { correlationId });
  417 |   } catch (e: unknown) {
  418 |     console.error("[exegesis/comment/edit] POST failed", e);
  419 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 500, {
  420 |       ok: false,
  421 |       error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Unknown error.",
  422 |     });
  423 |   }
  424 | }

  161 | const EMPTY_BODY_RICH_JSON = JSON.stringify({

  170 | function validationError(
  171 |   status: number,
  172 |   error: string,
  173 | ): ValidationResult<never> {
  174 |   return { ok: false, status, error };
  175 | }
  176 | 
  177 | function clampInt(v: unknown, min: number, max: number): number | null {
  178 |   if (typeof v !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(v)) return null;
  179 |   const n = Math.trunc(v);
  180 |   if (n < min) return min;
  181 |   if (n > max) return max;
  182 |   return n;
  183 | }
  184 | 
  185 | function normNullableId(v: unknown): string | null {
  186 |   const value = normString(v);
  187 |   if (!value || value === "null" || value === "undefined") return null;
  188 |   return value;
  189 | }

  191 | async function requireCanPost(memberId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  192 |   return await hasAnyEntitlement(memberId, [
  193 |     ENTITLEMENTS.TIER_PATRON,
  194 |     ENTITLEMENTS.TIER_PARTNER,
  195 |   ]);
  196 | }

  198 | function parseCommentContent(
  199 |   body: Record<string, unknown>,
  200 | ): ValidationResult<CommentContent> {
  201 |   const legacyBodyPlain = normString(body.bodyPlain);
  202 |   const bodyRichInput = "bodyRich" in body ? (body.bodyRich ?? null) : null;
  203 | 
  204 |   if (bodyRichInput === null || typeof bodyRichInput === "undefined") {
  205 |     if (!legacyBodyPlain) return validationError(400, "Missing bodyPlain.");
  206 |     if (legacyBodyPlain.length > 5000) {
  207 |       return validationError(400, "bodyPlain too long.");
  208 |     }
  209 | 
  210 |     return {
  211 |       ok: true,
  212 |       value: {
  213 |         bodyPlain: legacyBodyPlain,
  214 |         bodyRichJson: EMPTY_BODY_RICH_JSON,
  215 |       },
  216 |     };
  217 |   }
  218 | 
  219 |   const validated = validateAndSanitizeTipTapDoc(bodyRichInput);
  220 |   if (!validated.ok) return validationError(400, validated.error);
  221 | 
  222 |   let bodyRichJson = "";
  223 |   try {
  224 |     bodyRichJson = JSON.stringify(validated.doc);
  225 |   } catch {
  226 |     return validationError(400, "Invalid bodyRich.");
  227 |   }
  228 | 
  229 |   if (bodyRichJson.length > 200_000) {
  230 |     return validationError(400, "bodyRich too large.");
  231 |   }
  232 | 
  233 |   return {
  234 |     ok: true,
  235 |     value: {
  236 |       bodyPlain: validated.plain,
  237 |       bodyRichJson,
  238 |     },
  239 |   };
  240 | }
  241 | 
  242 | function parseCommentCommandDraft(
  243 |   raw: unknown,
  244 | ): ValidationResult<CommentCommandDraft> {
  245 |   const body = bodyRecord(raw);
  246 |   if (!body) return validationError(400, "Invalid JSON body.");
  247 | 
  248 |   const content = parseCommentContent(body);
  249 |   if (!content.ok) return content;
  250 | 
  251 |   const recordingId = normString(body.recordingId);
  252 |   if (!recordingId) return validationError(400, "Missing recordingId.");
  253 | 
  254 |   const lineKey = normString(body.lineKey);
  255 |   if (!lineKey) return validationError(400, "Missing lineKey.");
  256 | 
  257 |   return {
  258 |     ok: true,
  259 |     value: {
  260 |       ...content.value,
  261 |       recordingId,
  262 |       lineKey,
  263 |       groupKeyClient: normString(body.groupKey),
  264 |       parentId: normNullableId(body.parentId),
  265 |       lineTextSnapshot: normString(body.lineTextSnapshot),
  266 |       lyricsVersion: normString(body.lyricsVersion) || null,
  267 |       tMs: clampInt(body.tMs, 0, 60 * 60 * 1000),
  268 |     },
  269 |   };
  270 | }
  271 | 
  272 | async function resolveCommentCommand(
  273 |   draft: CommentCommandDraft,
  274 | ): Promise<ValidationResult<CommentCommand>> {
  275 |   const resolved = await resolveGroupKeyForAnchor({
  276 |     recordingId: draft.recordingId,
  277 |     lineKey: draft.lineKey,
  278 |   });
  279 |   const groupKey = resolved.groupKey;
  280 | 
  281 |   if (!groupKey) return validationError(400, "Could not resolve groupKey.");
  282 | 
  283 |   if (draft.groupKeyClient && draft.groupKeyClient !== groupKey) {
  284 |     return validationError(409, "Group key changed. Refresh and try again.");
  285 |   }
  286 | 
  287 |   if (!draft.bodyPlain) return validationError(400, "Missing bodyPlain.");
  288 |   if (draft.bodyPlain.length > 5000) {
  289 |     return validationError(400, "bodyPlain too long.");
  290 |   }
  291 |   if (!draft.lineTextSnapshot) {
  292 |     return validationError(400, "Missing lineTextSnapshot.");
  293 |   }
  294 |   if (draft.lineTextSnapshot.length > 2000) {
  295 |     return validationError(400, "lineTextSnapshot too long.");
  296 |   }
  297 |   if (draft.parentId && !isUuid(draft.parentId)) {
  298 |     return validationError(400, "Invalid parentId.");
  299 |   }
  300 | 
  301 |   return {
  302 |     ok: true,
  303 |     value: {
  304 |       recordingId: draft.recordingId,
  305 |       lineKey: draft.lineKey,
  306 |       groupKey,
  307 |       parentId: draft.parentId,
  308 |       bodyRichJson: draft.bodyRichJson,
  309 |       bodyPlain: draft.bodyPlain,
  310 |       lineTextSnapshot: draft.lineTextSnapshot,
  311 |       lyricsVersion: draft.lyricsVersion,
  312 |       tMs: draft.tMs,
  313 |     },
  314 |   };
  315 | }
  316 | 
  317 | async function readCommentCommand(
  318 |   req: NextRequest,
  319 | ): Promise<ValidationResult<CommentCommand>> {
  320 |   let raw: unknown;
  321 | 
  322 |   try {
  323 |     raw = await req.json();
  324 |   } catch {
  325 |     return validationError(400, "Invalid JSON body.");
  326 |   }
  327 | 
  328 |   const draft = parseCommentCommandDraft(raw);
  329 |   if (!draft.ok) return draft;
  330 | 
  331 |   return resolveCommentCommand(draft.value);
  332 | }

  334 | async function resolvePostingAuthority(
  335 |   req: NextRequest,
  336 |   correlationId: string,
  337 | ): Promise<PostingAuthorityResult> {
  338 |   const memberId = await requireExegesisMemberId();
  339 | 
  340 |   if (!memberId) {
  341 |     return {
  342 |       ok: false,
  343 |       response: gateError(req, {
  344 |         correlationId,
  345 |         status: 401,
  346 |         domain: "exegesis",
  347 |         code: "AUTH_REQUIRED",
  348 |         action: "login",
  349 |         message: "Sign in to post a comment.",
  350 |       }),
  351 |     };
  352 |   }
  353 | 
  354 |   if (!isUuid(memberId)) {
  355 |     return {
  356 |       ok: false,
  357 |       response: gateError(req, {
  358 |         correlationId,
  359 |         status: 403,
  360 |         domain: "exegesis",
  361 |         code: "PROVISIONING",
  362 |         action: "wait",
  363 |         message: "Still setting things up. Try again shortly.",
  364 |       }),
  365 |     };
  366 |   }
  367 | 
  368 |   const canPost = await requireCanPost(memberId);
  369 |   if (!canPost) {
  370 |     return {
  371 |       ok: false,
  372 |       response: gateError(req, {
  373 |         correlationId,
  374 |         status: 403,
  375 |         domain: "exegesis",
  376 |         code: "TIER_REQUIRED",
  377 |         action: "subscribe",
  378 |         message: "Posting requires Patron or Partner.",
  379 |       }),
  380 |     };
  381 |   }
  382 | 
  383 |   const canonicalIdentity = await ensureMemberIdentity(memberId);
  384 | 
  385 |   return {
  386 |     ok: true,
  387 |     memberId,
  388 |     identity: canonicalIdentity,
  389 |   };
  390 | }
  391 | 
  392 | async function insertComment(params: {
  393 |   command: CommentCommand;
  394 |   memberId: string;
  395 |   anonLabel: string;
  396 | }): Promise<CommentInsertRow | null> {
  397 |   const { command, memberId, anonLabel } = params;
  398 |   const {
  399 |     recordingId,
  400 |     groupKey,
  401 |     lineKey,
  402 |     parentId,
  403 |     bodyRichJson,
  404 |     bodyPlain,
  405 |     tMs,
  406 |     lineTextSnapshot,
  407 |     lyricsVersion,
  408 |   } = command;
  409 | 
  410 |   const rootIdForRootComment = crypto.randomUUID();
  411 |   const commentIdForReply = crypto.randomUUID();
  412 |   const parentUuid = parentId;
  413 | 
  414 |   const result = await sql<CommentInsertRow>`
  415 |       with
  416 | params as (
  417 |   select
  418 |     ${recordingId}::text          as track_id,
  419 |     ${groupKey}::text             as group_key,
  420 |     ${lineKey}::text              as line_key,
  421 |     nullif(${parentUuid}::text, '')::uuid as parent_id,
  422 |       ${memberId}::uuid             as member_id,
  423 |     ${anonLabel}::text            as anon_label,
  424 |     ${bodyRichJson}::jsonb        as body_rich,
  425 |     ${bodyPlain}::text            as body_plain,
  426 |     ${tMs}::int                   as t_ms,
  427 |     ${lineTextSnapshot}::text     as line_text_snapshot,
  428 |     ${lyricsVersion}::text        as lyrics_version,
  429 |     ${rootIdForRootComment}::uuid as root_id_for_root,
  430 |     ${commentIdForReply}::uuid    as id_for_reply
  431 | ),
  432 | meta_base as (
  433 |   insert into exegesis_thread_meta (track_id, group_key)
  434 |   select p.track_id, p.group_key
  435 |   from params p
  436 |   on conflict (track_id, group_key) do nothing
  437 |   returning track_id, group_key, pinned_comment_id, locked,
  438 |             comment_count, last_activity_at, created_at, updated_at
  439 | ),
  440 | meta_existing as (
  441 |   select m.track_id, m.group_key, m.pinned_comment_id, m.locked,
  442 |          m.comment_count, m.last_activity_at, m.created_at, m.updated_at
  443 |   from exegesis_thread_meta m
  444 |   join params p
  445 |     on p.track_id = m.track_id and p.group_key = m.group_key
  446 |   limit 1
  447 | ),
  448 | meta_pre as (
  449 |   select * from meta_base
  450 |   union all
  451 |   select * from meta_existing
  452 |   where not exists (select 1 from meta_base)
  453 | ),
  454 | ident_base as (
  455 |   insert into exegesis_identity (member_id, anon_label)
  456 |   select p.member_id, p.anon_label
  457 |   from params p
  458 |   on conflict (member_id) do nothing
  459 |   returning member_id, public_name_unlocked_at, contribution_count
  460 | ),
  461 | ident_existing as (
  462 |   select i.member_id, i.public_name_unlocked_at, i.contribution_count
  463 |   from exegesis_identity i
  464 |   join params p on p.member_id = i.member_id
  465 |   limit 1
  466 | ),
  467 | ident_pre as (
  468 |   select * from ident_base
  469 |   union all
  470 |   select * from ident_existing
  471 |   where not exists (select 1 from ident_base)
  472 | ),
  473 | parent_row as (
  474 |   select c.id, c.track_id, c.group_key, c.root_id, c.depth
  475 |   from exegesis_comment c
  476 |   join params p on c.id = p.parent_id
  477 |   limit 1
  478 | ),
  479 | parent_facts as (
  480 |   select
  481 |     p.parent_id is not null                          as has_parent,
  482 |     (select id from parent_row)                      as parent_id_found,
  483 |     (select track_id from parent_row)                as parent_track_id,
  484 |     (select group_key from parent_row)               as parent_group_key,
  485 |     (select root_id from parent_row)                 as parent_root_id,
  486 |     (select depth from parent_row)                   as parent_depth
  487 |   from params p
  488 | ),
  489 | guard_row as (
  490 |   select
  491 |     case
  492 |       when (select track_id from meta_pre) is null then 'META_MISSING'
  493 |       when (select member_id from ident_pre) is null then 'IDENT_MISSING'
  494 |       when (select locked from meta_pre) then 'LOCKED'
  495 |       when (select has_parent from parent_facts)
  496 |            and (select parent_id_found from parent_facts) is null then 'PARENT_NOT_FOUND'
  497 |       when (select has_parent from parent_facts)
  498 |            and (select parent_track_id from parent_facts) <> (select track_id from params) then 'PARENT_SCOPE'
  499 |       when (select has_parent from parent_facts)
  500 |            and (select parent_group_key from parent_facts) <> (select group_key from params) then 'PARENT_SCOPE'
  501 |       when (select has_parent from parent_facts)
  502 |            and ((select parent_depth from parent_facts) + 1) > 6 then 'DEPTH'
  503 |       else null
  504 |     end as err
  505 | ),
  506 | resolved as (
  507 |   select
  508 |     case
  509 |       when (select parent_id from params) is null then (select root_id_for_root from params)
  510 |       else (select parent_root_id from parent_facts)
  511 |     end as root_id,
  512 |     case
  513 |       when (select parent_id from params) is null then 0::int
  514 |       else ((select parent_depth from parent_facts) + 1)::int
  515 |     end as depth,
  516 |     case
  517 |       when (select parent_id from params) is null then (select root_id_for_root from params)
  518 |       else (select id_for_reply from params)
  519 |     end as id
  520 | ),
  521 | inserted as (
  522 |   insert into exegesis_comment (
  523 |     id, track_id, group_key, line_key, parent_id, root_id, depth,
  524 |     body_rich, body_plain, t_ms, line_text_snapshot, lyrics_version,
  525 |     created_by_member_id, status
  526 |   )
  527 |   select
  528 |     r.id,
  529 |     p.track_id,
  530 |     p.group_key,
  531 |     p.line_key,
  532 |     p.parent_id,
  533 |     r.root_id,
  534 |     r.depth,
  535 |     p.body_rich,
  536 |     p.body_plain,
  537 |     p.t_ms,
  538 |     p.line_text_snapshot,
  539 |     p.lyrics_version,
  540 |     p.member_id,
  541 |     'live'
  542 |   from params p
  543 |   cross join resolved r
  544 |   cross join guard_row g
  545 |   where g.err is null
  546 |   returning
  547 |     id, track_id, group_key, line_key, parent_id, root_id, depth,
  548 |     body_rich, body_plain, t_ms, line_text_snapshot, lyrics_version,
  549 |     created_by_member_id, status::text as status,
  550 |     created_at, edited_at, edit_count, vote_count
  551 | ),
  552 | meta_upd as (
  553 |   update exegesis_thread_meta m
  554 |   set
  555 |     comment_count = m.comment_count + 1,
  556 |     last_activity_at = now(),
  557 |     updated_at = now()
  558 |   from params p
  559 |   where m.track_id = p.track_id
  560 |     and m.group_key = p.group_key
  561 |     and exists (select 1 from inserted)
  562 |   returning m.track_id, m.group_key, m.pinned_comment_id, m.locked,
  563 |             m.comment_count, m.last_activity_at, m.created_at, m.updated_at
  564 | ),
  565 | ident_upd as (
  566 |   update exegesis_identity i
  567 |   set
  568 |     contribution_count = i.contribution_count + 1,
  569 |     public_name_unlocked_at = case
  570 |       when i.public_name_unlocked_at is null and (i.contribution_count + 1) >= 5 then now()
  571 |       else i.public_name_unlocked_at
  572 |     end,
  573 |     updated_at = now()
  574 |   from params p
  575 |   where i.member_id = p.member_id
  576 |     and exists (select 1 from inserted)
  577 |   returning i.member_id, i.public_name_unlocked_at, i.contribution_count
  578 | ),
  579 | meta_out as (
  580 |   select * from meta_upd
  581 |   union all
  582 |   select * from meta_pre
  583 |   where not exists (select 1 from meta_upd)
  584 | ),
  585 | ident_out as (
  586 |   select * from ident_upd
  587 |   union all
  588 |   select * from ident_pre
  589 |   where not exists (select 1 from ident_upd)
  590 | ),
  591 | stats as (
  592 |   select
  593 |     (select err from guard_row) as guard_err,
  594 |     (select count(*)::int from inserted) as inserted_count
  595 | )
  596 | select
  597 |   s.inserted_count,
  598 |   s.guard_err,
  599 |   i.id,
  600 |   i.track_id,
  601 |   i.group_key,
  602 |   i.line_key,
  603 |   i.parent_id,
  604 |   i.root_id,
  605 |   i.depth,
  606 |   i.body_rich,
  607 |   i.body_plain,
  608 |   i.t_ms,
  609 |   i.line_text_snapshot,
  610 |   i.lyrics_version,
  611 |   i.created_by_member_id,
  612 |   i.status,
  613 |   i.created_at,
  614 |   i.edited_at,
  615 |   i.edit_count,
  616 |   i.vote_count,
  617 |   m.track_id as meta_track_id,
  618 |   m.group_key as meta_group_key,
  619 |   m.pinned_comment_id as meta_pinned_comment_id,
  620 |   m.locked as meta_locked,
  621 |   m.comment_count as meta_comment_count,
  622 |   m.last_activity_at as meta_last_activity_at,
  623 |   m.created_at as meta_created_at,
  624 |   m.updated_at as meta_updated_at,
  625 |    u.member_id as ident_member_id,
  626 |   u.public_name_unlocked_at as ident_public_name_unlocked_at,
  627 |   u.contribution_count as ident_contribution_count
  628 | from stats s
  629 | join meta_out m on true
  630 | join ident_out u on true
  631 | left join inserted i on true
  632 | limit 1
  633 |   `;
  634 | 
  635 |   return result.rows?.[0] ?? null;
  636 | }
  637 | 
  638 | function guardFailureResponse(
  639 |   req: NextRequest,
  640 |   correlationId: string,
  641 |   guardError: string | null,
  642 | ): NextResponse {
  643 |   switch (guardError) {
  644 |     case "LOCKED":
  645 |       return gateError(req, {
  646 |         correlationId,
  647 |         status: 403,
  648 |         domain: "exegesis",
  649 |         code: "INVALID_REQUEST",
  650 |         action: "wait",
  651 |         message: "This thread is locked.",
  652 |       });
  653 |     case "PARENT_NOT_FOUND":
  654 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 404, {
  655 |         ok: false,
  656 |         error: "Parent not found.",
  657 |       });
  658 |     case "PARENT_SCOPE":
  659 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  660 |         ok: false,
  661 |         error: "Parent scope mismatch.",
  662 |       });
  663 |     case "DEPTH":
  664 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  665 |         ok: false,
  666 |         error: "Thread depth limit reached.",
  667 |       });
  668 |     case "META_MISSING":
  669 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 500, {
  670 |         ok: false,
  671 |         error: "Thread meta missing.",
  672 |       });
  673 |     case "IDENT_MISSING":
  674 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 500, {
  675 |         ok: false,
  676 |         error: "Identity missing.",
  677 |       });
  678 |     default:
  679 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 500, {
  680 |         ok: false,
  681 |         error: "Insert suppressed unexpectedly.",
  682 |       });
  683 |   }
  684 | }
  685 | 
  686 | function assertInsertedRow(
  687 |   row: CommentInsertRow,
  688 | ): asserts row is InsertedCommentRow {
  689 |   if (row.inserted_count !== 1) {
  690 |     throw new Error("assertInsertedRow: inserted_count != 1");
  691 |   }
  692 | 
  693 |   if (
  694 |     !row.id ||
  695 |     !row.track_id ||
  696 |     !row.group_key ||
  697 |     !row.line_key ||
  698 |     !row.root_id ||
  699 |     typeof row.depth !== "number" ||
  700 |     row.body_plain === null ||
  701 |     row.line_text_snapshot === null ||
  702 |     !row.created_by_member_id ||
  703 |     !row.status ||
  704 |     !row.created_at ||
  705 |     typeof row.edit_count !== "number" ||
  706 |     typeof row.vote_count !== "number"
  707 |   ) {
  708 |     throw new Error("assertInsertedRow: missing required comment fields");
  709 |   }
  710 | }
  711 | 
  712 | function resolveInsertResult(
  713 |   req: NextRequest,
  714 |   correlationId: string,
  715 |   row: CommentInsertRow | null,
  716 | ): InsertResolution {
  717 |   if (!row) {
  718 |     return {
  719 |       ok: false,
  720 |       response: jsonErr(correlationId, 500, {
  721 |         ok: false,
  722 |         error: "No response row.",
  723 |       }),
  724 |     };
  725 |   }
  726 | 
  727 |   if ((row.inserted_count ?? 0) === 0) {
  728 |     return {
  729 |       ok: false,
  730 |       response: guardFailureResponse(req, correlationId, row.guard_err),
  731 |     };
  732 |   }
  733 | 
  734 |   if (
  735 |     !row.id ||
  736 |     !row.track_id ||
  737 |     !row.group_key ||
  738 |     !row.line_key ||
  739 |     !row.root_id
  740 |   ) {
  741 |     return {
  742 |       ok: false,
  743 |       response: jsonErr(correlationId, 500, {
  744 |         ok: false,
  745 |         error: "Insert returned incomplete row.",
  746 |       }),
  747 |     };
  748 |   }
  749 | 
  750 |   assertInsertedRow(row);
  751 |   return { ok: true, row };
  752 | }
  753 |

  807 | async function runPostCommitBadgeEffects(params: {
  808 |   memberId: string;
  809 |   recordingId: string;
  810 |   publicNameUnlockedAt: string | null;
  811 |   correlationId: string;
  812 | }): Promise<NewlyAwardedBadge[]> {
  813 |   let badges: NewlyAwardedBadge[];
  814 | 
  815 |   try {
  816 |     badges = await runAutoBadgeAwardsForMember({
  817 |       memberId: params.memberId,
  818 |       trigger:
  819 |         params.publicNameUnlockedAt !== null
  820 |           ? "public_name_unlocked"
  821 |           : "exegesis_contribution_created",
  822 |       recordingId: params.recordingId,
  823 |       grantedBy: "system",
  824 |       correlationId: params.correlationId,
  825 |     });
  826 |   } catch (error: unknown) {
  827 |     console.error("[exegesis/comment] post-commit badge award failed", error);
  828 |     return [];
  829 |   }
  830 | 
  831 |   if (badges.length === 0) return badges;
  832 | 
  833 |   try {
  834 |     await markOverlayAnnouncedForAwardedBadges({
  835 |       memberId: params.memberId,
  836 |       badges,
  837 |     });
  838 |   } catch (error: unknown) {
  839 |     console.error(
  840 |       "[exegesis/comment] post-commit badge announcement failed",
  841 |       error,
  842 |     );
  843 |   }
  844 | 
  845 |   return badges;
  846 | }
  847 | 
  848 | function describeUnexpectedError(error: unknown): string {
  849 |   const details =
  850 |     typeof error === "object" && error !== null
  851 |       ? (error as {
  852 |           code?: string;
  853 |           message?: string;
  854 |           detail?: string;
  855 |           hint?: string;
  856 |         })
  857 |       : null;
  858 | 
  859 |   let message = "";
  860 |   if (typeof details?.message === "string" && details.message.trim()) {
  861 |     message = details.message.trim();
  862 |   } else if (error instanceof Error && error.message.trim()) {
  863 |     message = error.message.trim();
  864 |   }
  865 | 
  866 |   const extras = [details?.code, details?.detail, details?.hint].filter(
  867 |     (value): value is string =>
  868 |       typeof value === "string" && value.trim().length > 0,
  869 |   );
  870 | 
  871 |   return `${message || "Unknown error."}${
  872 |     extras.length > 0 ? ` (${extras.join(" · ")})` : ""
  873 |   }`;
  874 | }
  875 | 
  876 | export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  877 |   const correlationId = correlationIdFromRequest(req);
  878 | 
  879 |   const command = await readCommentCommand(req);
  880 |   if (!command.ok) {
  881 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, command.status, {
  882 |       ok: false,
  883 |       error: command.error,
  884 |     });
  885 |   }
  886 | 
  887 |   const authority = await resolvePostingAuthority(req, correlationId);
  888 |   if (!authority.ok) return authority.response;
  889 | 
  890 |   try {
  891 |     const insertRow = await insertComment({
  892 |       command: command.value,
  893 |       memberId: authority.memberId,
  894 |       anonLabel: authority.identity.anonLabel,
  895 |     });
  896 | 
  897 |     const insert = resolveInsertResult(req, correlationId, insertRow);
  898 |     if (!insert.ok) return insert.response;
  899 | 
  900 |     const { row } = insert;
  901 |     const identities = buildIdentityMap(row, authority.identity);
  902 |     const newlyAwardedBadges = await runPostCommitBadgeEffects({
  903 |       memberId: authority.memberId,
  904 |       recordingId: command.value.recordingId,
  905 |       publicNameUnlockedAt: row.ident_public_name_unlocked_at,
  906 |       correlationId,
  907 |     });
  908 | 
  909 |     return jsonOk<ApiOk>(
  910 |       {
  911 |         ok: true,
  912 |         recordingId: command.value.recordingId,
  913 |         groupKey: command.value.groupKey,
  914 |         comment: buildCommentDto(row),
  915 |         meta: buildThreadMetaDto(row),
  916 |         identities,
  917 |         newlyAwardedBadges,
  918 |       },
  919 |       { correlationId },
  920 |     );
  921 |   } catch (error: unknown) {
  922 |     console.error("[exegesis/comment] POST failed", error);
  923 | 
  924 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 500, {
  925 |       ok: false,
  926 |       error: describeUnexpectedError(error),
  927 |     });
  928 |   }
  929 | }

   28 | function jsonErr(correlationId: string, status: number, body: ApiErr) {
   29 |   return withCorrelationId(NextResponse.json(body, { status }), correlationId);

   32 | function norm(v: unknown): string {
   33 |   return typeof v === "string" ? v.trim() : "";
   34 | }
   35 | 
   36 | function isUuid(v: string): boolean {
   37 |   return /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-5][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(
   38 |     v,
   39 |   );

   42 | async function requireMemberId(): Promise<string | null> {
   43 |   const { userId } = await auth();
   44 |   if (!userId) return null;
   45 | 
   46 |   const r = await sql<{ id: string }>`
   47 |     select id
   48 |     from members
   49 |     where clerk_user_id = ${userId}
   50 |     limit 1
   51 |   `;
   52 |   const memberId = r.rows?.[0]?.id ?? "";
   53 |   return memberId || null;
   54 | }

   56 | async function requireCanReport(memberId: string): Promise<boolean> {
   57 |   return await hasAnyEntitlement(memberId, [
   58 |     ENTITLEMENTS.TIER_FRIEND,
   59 |     ENTITLEMENTS.TIER_PATRON,
   60 |     ENTITLEMENTS.TIER_PARTNER,
   61 |   ]);
   62 | }

   64 | const CATEGORIES = new Set([
   65 |   "spam",
   66 |   "harassment",
   67 |   "hate",
   68 |   "sexual",
   69 |   "self_harm",
   70 |   "violence",
   71 |   "misinfo",
   72 |   "copyright",
   73 |   "other",
   74 | ]);

   76 | function validateCategory(raw: string): string | null {
   77 |   const c = raw.trim().toLowerCase();
   78 |   return CATEGORIES.has(c) ? c : null;
   79 | }

   81 | export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
   82 |   const correlationId = correlationIdFromRequest(req);
   83 | 
   84 |   let raw: unknown;
   85 |   try {
   86 |     raw = await req.json();
   87 |   } catch {
   88 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
   89 |       ok: false,
   90 |       error: "Invalid JSON body.",
   91 |     });
   92 |   }
   93 | 
   94 |   const b =
   95 |     typeof raw === "object" && raw !== null
   96 |       ? (raw as Record<string, unknown>)
   97 |       : null;
   98 |   if (!b) {
   99 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  100 |       ok: false,
  101 |       error: "Invalid JSON body.",
  102 |     });
  103 |   }
  104 | 
  105 |   const commentId = norm(b.commentId);
  106 |   const categoryRaw = norm(b.category);
  107 |   const reason = norm(b.reason);
  108 | 
  109 |   if (!commentId) {
  110 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  111 |       ok: false,
  112 |       error: "Missing commentId.",
  113 |     });
  114 |   }
  115 |   if (!isUuid(commentId)) {
  116 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  117 |       ok: false,
  118 |       error: "Invalid commentId.",
  119 |     });
  120 |   }
  121 | 
  122 |   const category = validateCategory(categoryRaw);
  123 |   if (!category) {
  124 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  125 |       ok: false,
  126 |       error: "Invalid category.",
  127 |     });
  128 |   }
  129 | 
  130 |   if (reason.length < 20) {
  131 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  132 |       ok: false,
  133 |       error: "Reason must be at least 20 characters.",
  134 |     });
  135 |   }
  136 |   if (reason.length > 300) {
  137 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  138 |       ok: false,
  139 |       error: "Reason must be 300 characters or less.",
  140 |     });
  141 |   }
  142 | 
  143 |   const memberId = await requireMemberId();
  144 |   if (!memberId) {
  145 |     return gateError(req, {
  146 |       correlationId,
  147 |       status: 401,
  148 |       domain: "exegesis",
  149 |       code: "AUTH_REQUIRED",
  150 |       action: "login",
  151 |       message: "Sign in to report a comment.",
  152 |     });
  153 |   }
  154 | 
  155 |   if (!isUuid(memberId)) {
  156 |     return gateError(req, {
  157 |       correlationId,
  158 |       status: 403,
  159 |       domain: "exegesis",
  160 |       code: "PROVISIONING",
  161 |       action: "wait",
  162 |       message: "Provisioning required.",
  163 |     });
  164 |   }
  165 | 
  166 |   const canReport = await requireCanReport(memberId);
  167 |   if (!canReport) {
  168 |     return gateError(req, {
  169 |       correlationId,
  170 |       status: 403,
  171 |       domain: "exegesis",
  172 |       code: "TIER_REQUIRED",
  173 |       action: "subscribe",
  174 |       message: "Reporting requires Friend tier or higher.",
  175 |     });
  176 |   }
  177 | 
  178 |   try {
  179 |     const ins = await sql<{ id: string; comment_status: string | null }>`
  180 |       with c as (
  181 |         select id, status::text as status
  182 |         from exegesis_comment
  183 |         where id = ${commentId}::uuid
  184 |         limit 1
  185 |       ),
  186 |       inserted as (
  187 |         insert into exegesis_report (comment_id, reporter_member_id, category, reason)
  188 |         select
  189 |           c.id,
  190 |           ${memberId}::uuid,
  191 |           ${category},
  192 |           ${reason}
  193 |         from c
  194 |         where c.id is not null
  195 |           and c.status <> 'deleted'
  196 |           and not exists (
  197 |             select 1
  198 |             from exegesis_report r
  199 |             where r.comment_id = c.id
  200 |               and r.reporter_member_id = ${memberId}::uuid
  201 |           )
  202 |         returning id
  203 |       )
  204 |       select
  205 |         (select id from inserted limit 1) as id,
  206 |         (select status from c limit 1) as comment_status
  207 |     `;
  208 | 
  209 |     const reportId = ins.rows?.[0]?.id ?? "";
  210 |     const commentStatus = (ins.rows?.[0]?.comment_status ?? null) as
  211 |       | "live"
  212 |       | "hidden"
  213 |       | "deleted"
  214 |       | null;
  215 | 
  216 |     if (!commentStatus) {
  217 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 404, {
  218 |         ok: false,
  219 |         error: "Comment not found.",
  220 |       });
  221 |     }
  222 |     if (commentStatus === "deleted") {
  223 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  224 |         ok: false,
  225 |         error: "Cannot report a deleted comment.",
  226 |       });
  227 |     }
  228 | 
  229 |     if (!reportId) {
  230 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 409, {
  231 |         ok: false,
  232 |         code: "ALREADY_REPORTED",
  233 |         error: "You’ve already reported this comment.",
  234 |       });
  235 |     }
  236 | 
  237 |     return jsonOk<ApiOk>({ ok: true, reportId }, { correlationId });
  238 |   } catch (e: unknown) {
  239 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 500, {
  240 |       ok: false,
  241 |       error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Unknown error.",
  242 |     });
  243 |   }

  121 | async function getViewer(

  135 |   if (!memberId) return { kind: "anon", anonId: mintedAnonId };
  136 | 
  137 |   return { kind: "member", memberId };
  138 | }
