  191 | export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  192 |   const correlationId = correlationIdFromRequest(req);
  193 |   const url = new URL(req.url);
  194 | 
  195 |   const recordingId = norm(url.searchParams.get("recordingId"));
  196 |   const sortParam = norm(url.searchParams.get("sort")) || "top";
  197 |   const sort: ThreadSort = isSort(sortParam) ? sortParam : "top";
  198 | 
  199 |   if (!recordingId) {
  200 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  201 |       ok: false,
  202 |       error: "Missing recordingId.",
  203 |     });
  204 |   }
  205 |   if (recordingId.length > 200) {
  206 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  207 |       ok: false,
  208 |       error: "Invalid recordingId.",
  209 |     });
  210 |   }
  211 | 
  212 |   const rawGroupKey = norm(url.searchParams.get("groupKey"));
  213 |   const lineKey = norm(url.searchParams.get("lineKey"));
  214 | 
  215 |   let groupKey = "";
  216 | 
  217 |   if (lineKey) {
  218 |     const resolved = await resolveGroupKeyForAnchor({ recordingId, lineKey });
  219 |     groupKey = resolved.groupKey;
  220 | 
  221 |     if (rawGroupKey && norm(rawGroupKey) !== groupKey) {
  222 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 409, {
  223 |         ok: false,
  224 |         error: "Group key changed. Refresh and try again.",
  225 |       });
  226 |     }
  227 |   } else {
  228 |     if (!rawGroupKey) {
  229 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  230 |         ok: false,
  231 |         error: "Missing lineKey (or groupKey).",
  232 |       });
  233 |     }
  234 | 
  235 |     const g = norm(rawGroupKey);
  236 | 
  237 |     if (isGroupKeyV1(g)) {
  238 |       groupKey = g;
  239 |     } else {
  240 |       const ok = await isKnownCanonicalGroupKey({ recordingId, groupKey: g });
  241 |       if (!ok) {
  242 |         return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  243 |           ok: false,
  244 |           error: "Unknown groupKey.",
  245 |         });
  246 |       }
  247 |       groupKey = g;
  248 |     }
  249 |   }
  250 | 
  251 |   if (!groupKey) {
  252 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  253 |       ok: false,
  254 |       error: "Invalid group key.",
  255 |     });
  256 |   }
  257 | 
  258 |   const bootstrapRes = jsonOk({ ok: true }, { correlationId });
  259 |   const ensured = ensureAnonId(req, bootstrapRes);
  260 |   const { anonId } = ensured;
  261 | 
  262 |   const viewer = await getViewer(req, anonId);
  263 | 
  264 |   const viewerMemberId =
  265 |     viewer.kind === "member" && isUuid(viewer.memberId)
  266 |       ? viewer.memberId
  267 |       : null;
  268 | 
  269 |   if (viewerMemberId) {
  270 |     await ensureMemberIdentity(viewerMemberId);
  271 |   }
  272 | 
  273 |   if (viewer.kind === "anon") {
  274 |     const LIMIT = 8;
  275 |     const sessionId = anonId;
  276 | 
  277 |     await sql`
  278 |       insert into anon_exegesis_sessions (id, anon_id)
  279 |       values (${sessionId}, ${anonId})
  280 |       on conflict (id) do nothing
  281 |     `;
  282 | 
  283 |     const gateRes = await sql<{
  284 |       already: boolean;
  285 |       allowed: boolean;
  286 |       n_before: number;
  287 |       n_after: number;
  288 |     }>`
  289 |       with
  290 |       already as (
  291 |         select exists(
  292 |           select 1
  293 |           from anon_exegesis_thread_opens
  294 |           where session_id = ${sessionId}
  295 |             and track_id = ${recordingId}
  296 |             and group_key = ${groupKey}
  297 |         ) as already
  298 |       ),
  299 |       cnt_before as (
  300 |         select count(*)::int as n_before
  301 |         from anon_exegesis_thread_opens
  302 |         where session_id = ${sessionId}
  303 |       ),
  304 |       attempt as (
  305 |         insert into anon_exegesis_thread_opens (session_id, track_id, group_key)
  306 |         select ${sessionId}, ${recordingId}, ${groupKey}
  307 |         where (select already from already) = false
  308 |           and (select n_before from cnt_before) < ${LIMIT}
  309 |         on conflict (session_id, track_id, group_key) do nothing
  310 |         returning 1
  311 |       ),
  312 |       cnt_after as (
  313 |         select count(*)::int as n_after
  314 |         from anon_exegesis_thread_opens
  315 |         where session_id = ${sessionId}
  316 |       )
  317 |       select
  318 |         (select already from already) as already,
  319 |         ((select already from already) = true or exists(select 1 from attempt)) as allowed,
  320 |         (select n_before from cnt_before) as n_before,
  321 |         (select n_after from cnt_after) as n_after
  322 |     `;
  323 | 
  324 |     const row = gateRes.rows?.[0];
  325 |     const allowed = Boolean(row?.allowed);
  326 | 
  327 |     if (!allowed) {
  328 |       const message = "Sign in to keep reading.";
  329 | 
  330 |       return gateError(req, {
  331 |         correlationId,
  332 |         status: 403,
  333 |         domain: "exegesis",
  334 |         code: "EXEGESIS_THREAD_READ_CAP_REACHED",
  335 |         action: "login",
  336 |         message,
  337 |         onResponse: (res) => {
  338 |           for (const [k, v] of bootstrapRes.headers.entries()) {
  339 |             if (k.toLowerCase() === "set-cookie") res.headers.append(k, v);
  340 |           }
  341 |         },
  342 |       });
  343 |     }
  344 |   }
  345 | 
  346 |   const metaRes = await sql<DbThreadMetaRow>`
  347 |     select track_id, group_key, pinned_comment_id, locked, comment_count, last_activity_at, created_at, updated_at
  348 |     from exegesis_thread_meta
  349 |     where track_id = ${recordingId}
  350 |       and group_key = ${groupKey}
  351 |     limit 1
  352 |   `;
  353 |   const metaRow = metaRes.rows?.[0] ?? null;
  354 | 
  355 |   const commentsRes = await sql<DbCommentRow>`
  356 |     with base as (
  357 |       select
  358 |         c.id,
  359 |         c.track_id,
  360 |         c.group_key,
  361 |         c.line_key,
  362 |         c.parent_id,
  363 |         c.root_id,
  364 |         c.depth,
  365 |         c.body_rich,
  366 |         c.body_plain,
  367 |         c.t_ms,
  368 |         c.line_text_snapshot,
  369 |         c.lyrics_version,
  370 |         c.created_by_member_id,
  371 |         c.status::text as status,
  372 |         c.created_at,
  373 |         c.edited_at,
  374 |         c.edit_count,
  375 |         c.vote_count
  376 |       from exegesis_comment c
  377 |       where c.track_id = ${recordingId}
  378 |         and c.group_key = ${groupKey}
  379 |         and c.status <> 'deleted'
  380 |     ),
  381 |     voted as (
  382 |       select
  383 |         b.*,
  384 |         case
  385 |           when ${viewerMemberId}::uuid is null then false
  386 |           else exists (
  387 |             select 1
  388 |             from exegesis_vote v
  389 |             where v.member_id = ${viewerMemberId}::uuid
  390 |               and v.comment_id = b.id
  391 |           )
  392 |         end as viewer_has_voted
  393 |       from base b
  394 |     )
  395 |     select *
  396 |     from voted
  397 |     order by
  398 |       case when ${sort} = 'top' then (case when parent_id is null then vote_count else 0 end) end desc nulls last,
  399 |       case when ${sort} = 'recent' then (case when parent_id is null then created_at else null end) end desc nulls last,
  400 |       root_id asc,
  401 |       created_at asc
  402 |   `;
  403 | 
  404 |   const rows = commentsRes.rows ?? [];
  405 |   const byRoot = new Map<string, CommentDTO[]>();
  406 |   const authorIds = new Set<string>();
  407 | 
  408 |   for (const r of rows) {
  409 |     authorIds.add(r.created_by_member_id);
  410 | 
  411 |     const dto: CommentDTO = {
  412 |       id: r.id,
  413 |       recordingId: r.track_id,
  414 |       groupKey: r.group_key,
  415 |       lineKey: r.line_key,
  416 |       parentId: r.parent_id,
  417 |       rootId: r.root_id,
  418 |       depth: r.depth,
  419 |       bodyRich: r.body_rich,
  420 |       bodyPlain: r.body_plain,
  421 |       tMs: r.t_ms,
  422 |       lineTextSnapshot: r.line_text_snapshot,
  423 |       lyricsVersion: r.lyrics_version,
  424 |       createdByMemberId: r.created_by_member_id,
  425 |       status: r.status,
  426 |       createdAt: r.created_at,
  427 |       editedAt: r.edited_at,
  428 |       editCount: r.edit_count,
  429 |       voteCount: r.vote_count,
  430 |       viewerHasVoted: r.viewer_has_voted,
  431 |     };
  432 | 
  433 |     const arr = byRoot.get(dto.rootId) ?? [];
  434 |     arr.push(dto);
  435 |     byRoot.set(dto.rootId, arr);
  436 |   }
  437 | 
  438 |   const identities: Record<string, IdentityDTO> = {};
  439 |   const wantIds = new Set<string>();
  440 | 
  441 |   for (const id of Array.from(authorIds)) {
  442 |     const s = (id ?? "").trim();
  443 |     if (isUuid(s)) wantIds.add(s);
  444 |   }
  445 |   if (viewer.kind === "member") {
  446 |     const me = (viewer.memberId ?? "").trim();
  447 |     if (isUuid(me)) wantIds.add(me);
  448 |   }
  449 | 
  450 |   const uuids = Array.from(wantIds);
  451 | 
  452 |   if (uuids.length > 0) {
  453 |     const built = await buildExegesisIdentityDtoMap(uuids);
  454 |     for (const [memberId, identity] of Object.entries(built)) {
  455 |       identities[memberId] = identity;
  456 |     }
  457 |   }
  458 | 
  459 |   const roots = Array.from(byRoot.entries()).map(([rootId, comments]) => ({
  460 |     rootId,
  461 |     comments,
  462 |   }));
  463 | 
  464 |   const meta: ThreadMetaDTO | null = metaRow
  465 |     ? {
  466 |         recordingId: metaRow.track_id,
  467 |         groupKey: metaRow.group_key,
  468 |         pinnedCommentId: metaRow.pinned_comment_id,
  469 |         locked: metaRow.locked,
  470 |         commentCount: metaRow.comment_count,
  471 |         lastActivityAt: metaRow.last_activity_at,
  472 |         createdAt: metaRow.created_at,
  473 |         updatedAt: metaRow.updated_at,
  474 |       }
  475 |     : null;
  476 | 
  477 |   let viewerDto: ViewerDTO;
  478 | 
  479 |   if (!viewerMemberId) {
  480 |     viewerDto = { kind: "anon" };
  481 |   } else {
  482 |     const cap0 = await capsForMember(viewerMemberId);
  483 |     const locked = Boolean(metaRow?.locked);
  484 |     const me = identities[viewerMemberId];
  485 |     const canClaimName =
  486 |       !!me && !me.publicName && me.publicNameUnlockedAt != null;
  487 | 
  488 |     viewerDto = {
  489 |       kind: "member",
  490 |       memberId: viewerMemberId,
  491 |       cap: {
  492 |         ...cap0,
  493 |         canPost: locked ? false : cap0.canPost,
  494 |         canClaimName,
  495 |       },
  496 |     };
  497 |   }
  498 | 
  499 |   const okRes = jsonOk<ApiOk>(
  500 |     {
  501 |       ok: true,
  502 |       recordingId,
  503 |       groupKey,
  504 |       sort,
  505 |       meta,
  506 |       roots,
  507 |       identities,
  508 |       viewer: viewerDto,
  509 |     },
  510 |     { correlationId },
  511 |   );
  512 | 
  513 |   for (const [k, v] of bootstrapRes.headers.entries()) {
  514 |     if (k.toLowerCase() === "set-cookie") okRes.headers.append(k, v);
  515 |   }
  516 | 
  517 |   return okRes;
  518 | }

   71 | export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
   72 |   const correlationId = correlationIdFromRequest(req);
   73 | 
   74 |   let raw: unknown;
   75 |   try {
   76 |     raw = await req.json();

   78 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
   79 |       ok: false,
   80 |       error: "Invalid JSON body.",
   81 |     });
   82 |   }

   84 |   const b =
   85 |     typeof raw === "object" && raw !== null
   86 |       ? (raw as Record<string, unknown>)
   87 |       : null;
   88 |   if (!b) {
   89 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
   90 |       ok: false,
   91 |       error: "Invalid JSON body.",
   92 |     });
   93 |   }

   96 |   if (!commentId) {
   97 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
   98 |       ok: false,
   99 |       error: "Missing commentId.",
  100 |     });
  101 |   }
  102 |   if (!isUuid(commentId)) {
  103 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  104 |       ok: false,
  105 |       error: "Invalid commentId.",
  106 |     });
  107 |   }
  108 |

  122 |     return gateError(req, {
  123 |       correlationId,
  124 |       status: 403,
  125 |       domain: "exegesis",
  126 |       code: "PROVISIONING",
  127 |       action: "wait",
  128 |       message: "Provisioning required.",
  129 |     });
  130 |   }
  131 | 
  132 |   const canVote = await requireCanVote(memberId);
  133 |   if (!canVote) {
  134 |     return gateError(req, {
  135 |       correlationId,
  136 |       status: 403,
  137 |       domain: "exegesis",
  138 |       code: "TIER_REQUIRED",
  139 |       action: "subscribe",
  140 |       message: "Voting requires Friend tier or higher.",
  141 |     });
  142 |   }
  143 | 
  144 |   try {
  145 |     const r = await sql<{
  146 |       ok: boolean;
  147 |       viewer_has_voted: boolean;
  148 |       vote_count: number;
  149 |       err: string | null;
  150 |       author_member_id: string | null;
  151 |     }>`
  152 |       with
  153 |       c as (
  154 |         select
  155 |           id,
  156 |           status::text as status,
  157 |           track_id,
  158 |           group_key,
  159 |           vote_count,
  160 |           created_by_member_id
  161 |         from exegesis_comment
  162 |         where id = ${commentId}::uuid
  163 |         limit 1
  164 |       ),
  165 |       m as (
  166 |         select locked
  167 |         from exegesis_thread_meta
  168 |         where track_id = (select track_id from c)
  169 |           and group_key = (select group_key from c)
  170 |         limit 1
  171 |       ),
  172 |       guard as (
  173 |         select
  174 |           case
  175 |             when (select id from c) is null then 'NOT_FOUND'
  176 |             when (select status from c) = 'deleted' then 'DELETED'
  177 |             when (select status from c) = 'hidden' then 'HIDDEN'
  178 |             when coalesce((select locked from m), false) = true then 'LOCKED'
  179 |             else null
  180 |           end as err
  181 |       ),
  182 |       del as (
  183 |         delete from exegesis_vote
  184 |         where member_id = ${memberId}::uuid
  185 |           and comment_id = ${commentId}::uuid
  186 |           and (select err from guard) is null
  187 |         returning 1 as deleted
  188 |       ),
  189 |       ins as (
  190 |         insert into exegesis_vote (member_id, comment_id)
  191 |         select ${memberId}::uuid, ${commentId}::uuid
  192 |         where (select err from guard) is null
  193 |           and not exists (select 1 from del)
  194 |         on conflict (member_id, comment_id) do nothing
  195 |         returning 1 as inserted
  196 |       ),
  197 |       upd as (
  198 |         update exegesis_comment
  199 |         set vote_count = greatest(
  200 |           vote_count + (case when exists (select 1 from ins) then 1 else 0 end)
  201 |                      - (case when exists (select 1 from del) then 1 else 0 end),
  202 |           0
  203 |         )
  204 |         where id = ${commentId}::uuid
  205 |           and (select err from guard) is null
  206 |         returning vote_count
  207 |       )
  208 |        select
  209 |         (select err from guard) is null as ok,
  210 |         case
  211 |           when (select err from guard) is not null then false
  212 |           when exists (select 1 from ins) then true
  213 |           else false
  214 |         end as viewer_has_voted,
  215 |         coalesce((select vote_count from upd), (select vote_count from c), 0)::int as vote_count,
  216 |         (select err from guard) as err,
  217 |         (select created_by_member_id::text from c) as author_member_id
  218 |     `;
  219 | 
  220 |     const row = r.rows?.[0] ?? null;
  221 |     if (!row) {
  222 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 500, {
  223 |         ok: false,
  224 |         error: "Vote failed.",
  225 |       });
  226 |     }
  227 | 
  228 |     if (!row.ok) {
  229 |       if (row.err === "NOT_FOUND") {
  230 |         return jsonErr(correlationId, 404, {
  231 |           ok: false,
  232 |           error: "Comment not found.",
  233 |         });
  234 |       }
  235 |       if (row.err === "DELETED") {
  236 |         return gateError(req, {
  237 |           correlationId,
  238 |           status: 400,
  239 |           domain: "exegesis",
  240 |           code: "INVALID_REQUEST",
  241 |           action: "wait",
  242 |           message: "Cannot vote on deleted comment.",
  243 |         });
  244 |       }
  245 |       if (row.err === "HIDDEN") {
  246 |         return gateError(req, {
  247 |           correlationId,
  248 |           status: 403,
  249 |           domain: "exegesis",
  250 |           code: "INVALID_REQUEST",
  251 |           action: "wait",
  252 |           message: "Cannot vote on hidden comment.",
  253 |         });
  254 |       }
  255 |       if (row.err === "LOCKED") {
  256 |         return gateError(req, {
  257 |           correlationId,
  258 |           status: 403,
  259 |           domain: "exegesis",
  260 |           code: "INVALID_REQUEST",
  261 |           action: "wait",
  262 |           message: "Thread is locked.",
  263 |         });
  264 |       }
  265 | 
  266 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  267 |         ok: false,
  268 |         error: "Cannot vote on this comment.",
  269 |       });
  270 |     }
  271 | 
  272 |     const newlyAwardedBadges =
  273 |       row.author_member_id && isUuid(row.author_member_id)
  274 |         ? await runAutoBadgeAwardsForMember({
  275 |             memberId: row.author_member_id,
  276 |             trigger: "exegesis_vote_updated",
  277 |             grantedBy: "system",
  278 |             correlationId,
  279 |           })
  280 |         : [];
  281 | 
  282 |     if (row.author_member_id && isUuid(row.author_member_id)) {
  283 |       await markOverlayAnnouncedForAwardedBadges({
  284 |         memberId: row.author_member_id,
  285 |         badges: newlyAwardedBadges,
  286 |       });
  287 |     }
  288 | 
  289 |     return jsonOk<ApiOk>(
  290 |       {
  291 |         ok: true,
  292 |         commentId,
  293 |         viewerHasVoted: row.viewer_has_voted,
  294 |         voteCount: Number(row.vote_count ?? 0),
  295 |         newlyAwardedBadges,
  296 |       },
  297 |       { correlationId },
  298 |     );
  299 |   } catch (e: unknown) {
  300 |     const msg =
  301 |       e instanceof Error
  302 |         ? norm(e.message)
  303 |         : norm(typeof e === "string" ? e : "");
  304 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 500, {
  305 |       ok: false,
  306 |       error: msg || "Unknown error.",
  307 |     });

  309 | }

   34 |   return withCorrelationId(NextResponse.json(body, { status }), correlationId);
   35 | }
   36 | 
   37 | function norm(v: unknown): string {
   38 |   return typeof v === "string" ? v.trim() : "";
   39 | }
   40 | 
   41 | function validatePublicName(
   42 |   raw: string,
   43 | ): { ok: true; value: string; lowered: string } | { ok: false; error: string } {
   44 |   const s = raw.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
   45 | 
   46 |   if (s.length < 3) {
   47 |     return { ok: false, error: "Name must be at least 3 characters." };
   48 |   }
   49 |   if (s.length > 32) {
   50 |     return { ok: false, error: "Name must be 32 characters or less." };
   51 |   }
   52 | 
   53 |   if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9 _.'-]*[A-Za-z0-9]$/.test(s)) {
   54 |     return { ok: false, error: "Name contains invalid characters." };
   55 |   }
   56 | 
   57 |   if (/[_.\-']{3,}/.test(s)) {
   58 |     return { ok: false, error: "Name contains too much punctuation." };
   59 |   }
   60 | 
   61 |   const lowered = s.toLowerCase();
   62 | 
   63 |   const reserved = new Set(
   64 |     [
   65 |       "admin",
   66 |       "administrator",
   67 |       "support",
   68 |       "moderator",
   69 |       "mod",
   70 |       "system",
   71 |       "anonymous",
   72 |       "null",
   73 |       "undefined",
   74 |       "bjr",
   75 |       "brendan john roch",
   76 |       "angelfish records",
   77 |       "angelfish records official",
   78 |       "brendan john roch official",
   79 |       "bjr official",
   80 |     ].map((x) => x.toLowerCase()),
   81 |   );
   82 | 
   83 |   if (reserved.has(lowered)) {
   84 |     return { ok: false, error: "That name is reserved." };
   85 |   }
   86 | 
   87 |   return { ok: true, value: s, lowered };
   88 | }
   89 | 
   90 | async function requireMemberId(): Promise<string | null> {
   91 |   const { userId } = await auth();
   92 |   if (!userId) return null;
   93 | 
   94 |   const r = await sql<{ id: string }>`
   95 |     select id
   96 |     from members
   97 |     where clerk_user_id = ${userId}
   98 |     limit 1
   99 |   `;
  100 | 
  101 |   return r.rows[0]?.id ?? null;
  102 | }

  104 | export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  105 |   const correlationId = correlationIdFromRequest(req);
  106 | 
  107 |   let raw: unknown;
  108 |   try {
  109 |     raw = await req.json();
  110 |   } catch {
  111 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  112 |       ok: false,
  113 |       error: "Invalid JSON body.",
  114 |     });
  115 |   }
  116 | 
  117 |   const body =
  118 |     typeof raw === "object" && raw !== null
  119 |       ? (raw as Record<string, unknown>)
  120 |       : null;
  121 | 
  122 |   if (!body) {
  123 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  124 |       ok: false,
  125 |       error: "Invalid JSON body.",
  126 |     });
  127 |   }
  128 | 
  129 |   const desiredRaw = norm(body.publicName);
  130 |   const v = validatePublicName(desiredRaw);
  131 | 
  132 |   if (!v.ok) {
  133 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 400, {
  134 |       ok: false,
  135 |       error: v.error,
  136 |     });
  137 |   }
  138 | 
  139 |   const memberId = await requireMemberId();
  140 | 
  141 |   if (!memberId) {
  142 |     return gateError(req, {
  143 |       correlationId,
  144 |       status: 401,
  145 |       domain: "site",
  146 |       code: "AUTH_REQUIRED",
  147 |       action: "login",
  148 |       message: "Sign in required.",
  149 |     });
  150 |   }
  151 | 
  152 |   const state = await buildMemberIdentityState(memberId);
  153 | 
  154 |   if (!state.capability.canClaimName) {
  155 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 403, {
  156 |       ok: false,
  157 |       code: "NOT_UNLOCKED",
  158 |       error: "Public name is not unlocked yet.",
  159 |     });
  160 |   }
  161 | 
  162 |   try {
  163 |     await sql`
  164 |       update member_identity
  165 |       set
  166 |         public_name = ${v.value}::citext,
  167 |         public_name_claimed_at = coalesce(public_name_claimed_at, now()),
  168 |         updated_at = now()
  169 |       where member_id = ${memberId}::uuid
  170 |     `;
  171 |   } catch (error: unknown) {
  172 |     const err = error as { code?: string } | null;
  173 | 
  174 |     if (err?.code === "23505") {
  175 |       return jsonErr(correlationId, 409, {
  176 |         ok: false,
  177 |         code: "TAKEN",
  178 |         error: "That name is already taken.",
  179 |       });
  180 |     }
  181 | 
  182 |     return jsonErr(correlationId, 500, {
  183 |       ok: false,
  184 |       error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown error.",
  185 |     });
  186 |   }
  187 | 
  188 |   const identity = await buildExegesisIdentityDto(memberId);
  189 | 
  190 |   return jsonOk<ApiOk>(
  191 |     {
  192 |       ok: true,
  193 |       identity,
  194 |     },
  195 |     { correlationId },
  196 |   );
  197 | }

   25 | function dedupeIncomingBadges(
   26 |   badges: BadgeAwardNotice[],
   27 |   recentSeenRef: React.MutableRefObject<Map<string, number>>,
   28 | ): BadgeAwardNotice[] {
   29 |   const now = Date.now();
   30 | 
   31 |   for (const [key, expiresAt] of recentSeenRef.current.entries()) {
   32 |     if (expiresAt <= now) {
   33 |       recentSeenRef.current.delete(key);
   34 |     }
   35 |   }
   36 | 
   37 |   const unique: BadgeAwardNotice[] = [];
   38 |   const seenThisBatch = new Set<string>();
   39 | 
   40 |   for (const badge of badges) {
   41 |     const key = badge.entitlementKey.trim();
   42 |     if (!key) continue;
   43 |     if (seenThisBatch.has(key)) continue;
   44 |     if (recentSeenRef.current.has(key)) continue;
   45 | 
   46 |     seenThisBatch.add(key);
   47 |     unique.push(badge);
   48 |   }
   49 | 
   50 |   return unique;
   51 | }
   52 |

   63 |   const dismissTimeoutRef = React.useRef<number | null>(null);
   64 |   const clearActiveTimeoutRef = React.useRef<number | null>(null);
   65 | 
   66 |   const clearTimers = React.useCallback(() => {
   67 |     if (dismissTimeoutRef.current !== null) {
   68 |       window.clearTimeout(dismissTimeoutRef.current);
   69 |       dismissTimeoutRef.current = null;
   70 |     }
   71 |

   74 |       clearActiveTimeoutRef.current = null;
   75 |     }
   76 |   }, []);
   77 | 
   78 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   79 |     return () => {
   80 |       clearTimers();
   81 |     };
   82 |   }, [clearTimers]);
   83 | 
   84 |   const dismissActive = React.useCallback(() => {

  124 |     setQueue(rest);
  125 |     setActiveBadge(nextBadge);
  126 |     setVisible(true);
  127 | 
  128 |     recentSeenRef.current.set(
  129 |       nextBadge.entitlementKey,
  130 |       Date.now() + RECENT_DEDUPE_WINDOW_MS,
  131 |     );
  132 | 
  133 |     dismissTimeoutRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
  134 |       setVisible(false);
  135 | 
  136 |       clearActiveTimeoutRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
  137 |         setActiveBadge(null);
  138 |         clearActiveTimeoutRef.current = null;
  139 |       }, EXIT_MS);
  140 | 
  141 |       dismissTimeoutRef.current = null;
  142 |     }, DISPLAY_MS);
  143 |   }, [activeBadge, queue]);
  144 | 
  145 |   const contextValue = React.useMemo<BadgeAwardOverlayContextValue>(
  146 |     () => ({
  147 |       announceBadge,
  148 |       announceBadges,
  149 |       resetOverlayDebugState,
  150 |     }),
  151 |     [announceBadge, announceBadges, resetOverlayDebugState],
  152 |   );
  153 | 
  154 |   return (
  155 |     <BadgeAwardOverlayContext.Provider value={contextValue}>

  157 |

    1 | export type BadgeAwardNotice = {
    2 |   entitlementKey: string;
    3 |   title: string;
    4 |   description: string | null;
    5 |   imageUrl: string | null;
    6 |   shareable: boolean;

    9 | 
   10 | function asTrimmedString(value: unknown): string | null {
   11 |   return typeof value === "string" && value.trim() ? value.trim() : null;
   12 | }
   13 | 
   14 | function asBoolean(value: unknown): boolean {
   15 |   return value === true;
   16 | }
   17 | 
   18 | function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
   19 |   return typeof value === "object" && value !== null;
   20 | }
   21 | 
   22 | export function normalizeBadgeAwardNotices(
   23 |   value: unknown,
   24 | ): BadgeAwardNotice[] {
   25 |   if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];
   26 | 
   27 |   const notices: BadgeAwardNotice[] = [];
   28 | 
   29 |   for (const item of value) {
   30 |     if (!isRecord(item)) continue;
   31 | 
   32 |     const entitlementKey = asTrimmedString(item.entitlementKey);
   33 |     const title = asTrimmedString(item.title);
   34 |     const unlockedAt = asTrimmedString(item.unlockedAt);
   35 | 
   36 |     if (!entitlementKey || !title || !unlockedAt) continue;
   37 | 
   38 |     notices.push({
   39 |       entitlementKey,
   40 |       title,
   41 |       description: asTrimmedString(item.description),
   42 |       imageUrl: asTrimmedString(item.imageUrl),
   43 |       shareable: asBoolean(item.shareable),
   44 |       unlockedAt,
   45 |     });
   46 |   }
   47 | 
   48 |   return notices;
   49 | }

    1 | //web/app/home/gating/GateBroker.tsx
    2 | "use client";
    3 | 
    4 | import React from "react";
    5 | import type {
    6 |   GateAction,
    7 |   GateCodeRaw,
    8 |   GateDomain,
    9 |   GateReason,
   10 |   GateUiMode,
   11 | } from "@/app/home/gating/gateTypes";
   12 | import { canonicalizeLegacyCapCode } from "@/app/home/gating/gateTypes";
   13 | 
   14 | export type GateReport = {
   15 |   code: GateCodeRaw;
   16 |   action: GateAction;
   17 |   message: string;
   18 |   domain: GateDomain;
   19 |   uiMode: GateUiMode; // REQUIRED: adapters must ask engine
   20 |   correlationId?: string | null;
   21 | };
   22 | 
   23 | export type GateState = {
   24 |   active: GateReason | null;
   25 |   uiMode: GateUiMode;
   26 |   lastSetAtMs: number | null;
   27 | };
   28 | 
   29 | type Ctx = {
   30 |   gate: GateState;
   31 |   reportGate: (r: GateReport) => void;
   32 |   clearGate: (opts?: { domain?: GateDomain }) => void;
   33 | };
   34 | 
   35 | const GateBrokerContext = React.createContext<Ctx | null>(null);
   36 | 
   37 | export function GateBrokerProvider(props: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
   38 |   const [gate, setGate] = React.useState<GateState>({
   39 |     active: null,
   40 |     uiMode: "none",
   41 |     lastSetAtMs: null,
   42 |   });
   43 | 
   44 |   const reportGate = React.useCallback((r: GateReport) => {
   45 |     const normalized = canonicalizeLegacyCapCode(r.code, r.domain);
   46 | 
   47 |     setGate((prev) => {
   48 |       const nextReason: GateReason = {
   49 |         code: normalized,
   50 |         action: r.action,
   51 |         message: r.message,
   52 |         correlationId: r.correlationId ?? null,
   53 |         domain: r.domain,
   54 |       };
   55 | 
   56 |       // Idempotent-ish: don’t churn state if nothing meaningful changed.
   57 |       const prevActive = prev.active;
   58 |       const same =
   59 |         prevActive &&
   60 |         prevActive.code === nextReason.code &&
   61 |         prevActive.action === nextReason.action &&
   62 |         prevActive.message === nextReason.message &&
   63 |         (prevActive.correlationId ?? null) ===
   64 |           (nextReason.correlationId ?? null) &&
   65 |         (prevActive.domain ?? "generic") === (nextReason.domain ?? "generic");
   66 | 
   67 |       if (same) return prev;
   68 | 
   69 |       if (!r.uiMode) {
   70 |         // This should be impossible once adapters are spine-aligned.
   71 |         // Keep the crash dev-only so prod sessions don't brick if an edge path regresses.
   72 |         if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production") {
   73 |           throw new Error(
   74 |             `[GateBroker] Missing uiMode in reportGate (domain=${r.domain}, code=${normalized})`,
   75 |           );
   76 |         }
   77 |       }
   78 | 
   79 |       return {
   80 |         active: nextReason,
   81 |         uiMode: r.uiMode ?? "inline",
   82 |         lastSetAtMs: Date.now(),
   83 |       };
   84 |     });
   85 |   }, []);
   86 | 
   87 |   const clearGate = React.useCallback((opts?: { domain?: GateDomain }) => {
   88 |     setGate((prev) => {
   89 |       if (!prev.active) return prev;
   90 |       if (opts?.domain && (prev.active.domain ?? "generic") !== opts.domain)
   91 |         return prev;
   92 |       return { active: null, uiMode: "none", lastSetAtMs: prev.lastSetAtMs };
   93 |     });
   94 |   }, []);
   95 | 
   96 |   const value = React.useMemo<Ctx>(
   97 |     () => ({ gate, reportGate, clearGate }),
   98 |     [gate, reportGate, clearGate],
   99 |   );
  100 | 
  101 |   return (
  102 |     <GateBrokerContext.Provider value={value}>
  103 |       {props.children}
  104 |     </GateBrokerContext.Provider>
  105 |   );
  106 | }
  107 | 
  108 | export function useGateBroker(): Ctx {
  109 |   const ctx = React.useContext(GateBrokerContext);
  110 |   if (!ctx) {
  111 |     throw new Error("useGateBroker must be used within GateBrokerProvider");
  112 |   }
  113 |   return ctx;
  114 | }

===== COMPLETE FILE: app/home/gating/fromPayload.ts =====
SHA256: fe625913b025a7cd18c7b7871fff6a853d8b7f361271d877f27e28fce022f1ce

    1 | // web/app/home/gating/fromPayload.ts
    2 | import { gate } from "@/app/home/gating/gate";
    3 | import type {
    4 |   GateAttempt,
    5 |   GateContext,
    6 |   GateResult,
    7 | } from "@/app/home/gating/gate";
    8 | import type { GatePayload, GateDomain } from "@/app/home/gating/gateTypes";
    9 | import { canonicalizeLegacyCapCode } from "@/app/home/gating/gateTypes";
   10 | 
   11 | /** Narrow unknown -> GatePayload (bare or wrapped). */
   12 | function extractGatePayload(raw: unknown): GatePayload | null {
   13 |   if (!raw || typeof raw !== "object") return null;
   14 | 
   15 |   // Wrapped: { ok:false; gate:{...} }
   16 |   const r = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
   17 |   if (r.ok === false && "gate" in r) {
   18 |     const g = r.gate as unknown;
   19 |     if (g && typeof g === "object") {
   20 |       const gg = g as Record<string, unknown>;
   21 |       if (
   22 |         typeof gg.code === "string" &&
   23 |         typeof gg.action === "string" &&
   24 |         typeof gg.domain === "string" &&
   25 |         typeof gg.message === "string"
   26 |       ) {
   27 |         return g as GatePayload;
   28 |       }
   29 |     }
   30 |   }
   31 | 
   32 |   // Bare payload: { code, action, domain, message, ... }
   33 |   if (
   34 |     typeof r.code === "string" &&
   35 |     typeof r.action === "string" &&
   36 |     typeof r.domain === "string" &&
   37 |     typeof r.message === "string"
   38 |   ) {
   39 |     return raw as GatePayload;
   40 |   }
   41 | 
   42 |   return null;
   43 | }
   44 | 
   45 | /** Canonicalize legacy CAP_* to domain-specific cap codes, plus hygiene. */
   46 | function normalizePayload(payload: GatePayload): GatePayload {
   47 |   const domain = (payload.domain ?? "unknown") as GateDomain;
   48 |   const code = canonicalizeLegacyCapCode(payload.code, domain);
   49 | 
   50 |   return {
   51 |     ...payload,
   52 |     domain,
   53 |     code,
   54 |     message: (payload.message ?? "").trim(),
   55 |     correlationId: payload.correlationId ?? null,
   56 |   };
   57 | }
   58 | 
   59 | function ctxFromCode(
   60 |   codeRaw: GatePayload["code"],
   61 |   domain: GatePayload["domain"],
   62 | ) {
   63 |   const code = canonicalizeLegacyCapCode(codeRaw, domain);
   64 | 
   65 |   return {
   66 |     readReceiptsCapReached: code === "READ_RECEIPTS_CAP_REACHED",
   67 |     playbackCapReached: code === "PLAYBACK_CAP_REACHED",
   68 |     journalReadCapReached: code === "JOURNAL_READ_CAP_REACHED",
   69 |     exegesisThreadReadCapReached: code === "EXEGESIS_THREAD_READ_CAP_REACHED",
   70 |     isEmbargoed: code === "EMBARGO",
   71 |     isProvisioning: code === "PROVISIONING",
   72 |     // tier/entitlement are “negative capability flags”
   73 |     hasTierAccess: code === "TIER_REQUIRED" ? false : undefined,
   74 |     hasEntitlement: code === "ENTITLEMENT_REQUIRED" ? false : undefined,
   75 |   } as const;
   76 | }
   77 | 
   78 | /**
   79 |  * Compute UI policy (uiMode + CTA) locally, based on gate() engine.
   80 |  * Server supplies only the reason (code/action/domain/message).
   81 |  *
   82 |  * This variant assumes you already have a GatePayload.
   83 |  */
   84 | export function gateResultFromPayload(opts: {
   85 |   payload: GatePayload;
   86 |   attempt: GateAttempt;
   87 |   isSignedIn?: boolean;
   88 |   intent: "passive" | "explicit";
   89 | }): GateResult {
   90 |   const payload = normalizePayload(opts.payload);
   91 | 
   92 |   const flags = ctxFromCode(payload.code, payload.domain);
   93 | 
   94 |   const ctx: GateContext = {
   95 |     isSignedIn: opts.isSignedIn ?? false,
   96 |     intent: opts.intent,
   97 |     ...flags,
   98 |   };
   99 | 
  100 |   const res = gate(opts.attempt, ctx);
  101 | 
  102 |   // Prefer server message when blocked (so copy can be server-authored),
  103 |   // but keep engine invariants for uiMode + CTA.
  104 |   if (!res.ok) {
  105 |     return {
  106 |       ...res,
  107 |       cta: {
  108 |         ...res.cta,
  109 |         action: payload.action || res.cta.action,
  110 |       },
  111 |       reason: {
  112 |         ...res.reason,
  113 |         // Keep engine code/domain/uiMode, but preserve server-authored action/message.
  114 |         action: payload.action || res.reason.action,
  115 |         message: payload.message || res.reason.message,
  116 |         correlationId: payload.correlationId ?? null,
  117 |       },
  118 |     };
  119 |   }
  120 | 
  121 |   return res;
  122 | }
  123 | 
  124 | /**
  125 |  * Convenience: accept unknown API JSON and (if it contains a gate) produce GateResult.
  126 |  * Returns null when no gate payload is present.
  127 |  */
  128 | export function gateResultFromUnknown(opts: {
  129 |   raw: unknown;
  130 |   attempt: GateAttempt;
  131 |   isSignedIn?: boolean;
  132 |   intent: "passive" | "explicit";
  133 | }): GateResult | null {
  134 |   const payload = extractGatePayload(opts.raw);
  135 |   if (!payload) return null;
  136 | 
  137 |   return gateResultFromPayload({
  138 |     payload,
  139 |     attempt: opts.attempt,
  140 |     isSignedIn: opts.isSignedIn,
  141 |     intent: opts.intent,
  142 |   });
  143 | }
  144 | 
  145 | /**
  146 |  * Convenience: extract + normalize just the payload (handy when you need to forward it).
  147 |  * Returns null when no gate payload is present.
  148 |  */
  149 | export function gatePayloadFromUnknown(raw: unknown): GatePayload | null {
  150 |   const payload = extractGatePayload(raw);
  151 |   return payload ? normalizePayload(payload) : null;
  152 | }

   65 | function defaultMessageFor(code: GateCode, verb: GateVerb): string {
   66 |   switch (code) {
   67 |     case "AUTH_REQUIRED":
   68 |       return "Sign in to continue.";
   69 |     case "ENTITLEMENT_REQUIRED":
   70 |       return verb === "download"
   71 |         ? "Downloads are for members."
   72 |         : "This is for members.";
   73 |     case "TIER_REQUIRED":
   74 |       return "Upgrade your membership to access this.";
   75 |     case "EMBARGO":
   76 |       return "This content isn’t available yet.";
   77 |     case "PROVISIONING":
   78 |       return "Still setting things up. Try again shortly.";
   79 |     case "READ_RECEIPTS_CAP_REACHED":
   80 |       return "You’ve reached the anonymous activity limit.";
   81 |     case "PLAYBACK_CAP_REACHED":
   82 |       return "Enter your email address to continue listening.";
   83 |     case "JOURNAL_READ_CAP_REACHED":
   84 |       return "You’ve reached the anonymous reading limit.";
   85 |     case "EXEGESIS_THREAD_READ_CAP_REACHED":
   86 |       return "Sign in to keep reading.";
   87 |     case "INVALID_REQUEST":
   88 |       return "That request couldn’t be processed.";
   89 |   }
   90 | }
   91 | 
   92 | function defaultActionFor(code: GateCode): GateAction {
   93 |   switch (code) {
   94 |     case "AUTH_REQUIRED":
   95 |     case "PLAYBACK_CAP_REACHED":
   96 |     case "EXEGESIS_THREAD_READ_CAP_REACHED":
   97 |       return "login";
   98 |     case "ENTITLEMENT_REQUIRED":
   99 |     case "TIER_REQUIRED":
  100 |     case "READ_RECEIPTS_CAP_REACHED":
  101 |     case "JOURNAL_READ_CAP_REACHED":
  102 |       return "subscribe";
  103 |     case "EMBARGO":
  104 |     case "PROVISIONING":
  105 |       return "wait";
  106 |     case "INVALID_REQUEST":
  107 |       return "wait";
  108 |   }
  109 | }
  110 | 
  111 | function defaultUiModeFor(
  112 |   domain: GateDomain,
  113 |   code: GateCode,
  114 |   verb: GateVerb,
  115 |   intent: "passive" | "explicit",
  116 | ): GateUiMode {
  117 |   // Invariants: receipts, journal, and exegesis read caps never spotlight (inline only).
  118 |   if (code === "READ_RECEIPTS_CAP_REACHED") return "inline";
  119 |   if (code === "JOURNAL_READ_CAP_REACHED") return "inline";
  120 |   if (code === "EXEGESIS_THREAD_READ_CAP_REACHED") return "inline";
  121 | 
  122 |   // Playback cap is the canonical blocking playback gate.
  123 |   // If a play attempt is being denied, surface the full-screen prompt even when
  124 |   // the denial arose from auto-advance rather than a fresh click/tap.
  125 |   if (
  126 |     domain === "playback" &&
  127 |     verb === "play" &&
  128 |     code === "PLAYBACK_CAP_REACHED"
  129 |   ) {
  130 |     return "spotlight";
  131 |   }
  132 | 
  133 |   // Primary intent blocks tend to be global (but non-spotlight).
  134 |   if (domain === "playback" && verb === "play") return "global";
  135 |   if (verb === "download") return "global";
  136 | 
  137 |   // Everything else defaults to inline (action-only gating).
  138 |   return "inline";
  139 | }
  140 | 
  141 | /**
  142 |  * Pure policy brain. No React, no side effects.
  143 |  * You can call this from adapters or from a future GateBroker.
  144 |  */
  145 | export function gate(attempt: GateAttempt, ctx: GateContext): GateResult {
  146 |   const intent = ctx.intent ?? "passive";
  147 | 
  148 |   // Verbs that are allowed for anonymous users (product invariant: “read stays broadly available”).
  149 |   // You can tighten/expand this list per module over time.
  150 |   const anonAllowedVerb: boolean =
  151 |     attempt.verb === "play" ||
  152 |     attempt.verb === "readFullThread" ||
  153 |     attempt.verb === "markSeen";
  154 | 
  155 |   // 1) Embargo / provisioning are domain-agnostic “wait” gates.
  156 |   if (ctx.isEmbargoed) {
  157 |     const code: GateCode = "EMBARGO";
  158 |     const action = defaultActionFor(code);
  159 |     return {
  160 |       ok: false,
  161 |       uiMode: defaultUiModeFor(attempt.domain, code, attempt.verb, intent),
  162 |       cta: { action, label: "Not yet" },
  163 |       reason: {
  164 |         code,
  165 |         action,
  166 |         message: defaultMessageFor(code, attempt.verb),
  167 |         domain: attempt.domain,
  168 |       },
  169 |     };
  170 |   }
  171 | 
  172 |   if (ctx.isProvisioning) {
  173 |     const code: GateCode = "PROVISIONING";
  174 |     const action = defaultActionFor(code);
  175 |     return {
  176 |       ok: false,
  177 |       uiMode: defaultUiModeFor(attempt.domain, code, attempt.verb, intent),
  178 |       cta: { action, label: "Try again" },
  179 |       reason: {
  180 |         code,
  181 |         action,
  182 |         message: defaultMessageFor(code, attempt.verb),
  183 |         domain: attempt.domain,
  184 |       },
  185 |     };
  186 |   }
  187 | 
  188 |   // 2) Caps (domain explicit). These can apply to anonymous users.
  189 |   if (attempt.domain === "playback" && ctx.playbackCapReached) {
  190 |     const code: GateCode = "PLAYBACK_CAP_REACHED";
  191 |     const action = defaultActionFor(code);
  192 |     return {
  193 |       ok: false,
  194 |       uiMode: defaultUiModeFor(attempt.domain, code, attempt.verb, intent),
  195 |       cta: { action, label: "Unlock playback" },
  196 |       reason: {
  197 |         code,
  198 |         action,
  199 |         message: defaultMessageFor(code, attempt.verb),
  200 |         domain: attempt.domain,
  201 |       },
  202 |     };
  203 |   }
  204 | 
  205 |   if (attempt.domain === "journal" && ctx.journalReadCapReached) {
  206 |     const code: GateCode = "JOURNAL_READ_CAP_REACHED";
  207 |     const action = defaultActionFor(code);
  208 |     return {
  209 |       ok: false,
  210 |       uiMode: defaultUiModeFor(attempt.domain, code, attempt.verb, intent),
  211 |       cta: { action, label: "Unlock journal" },
  212 |       reason: {
  213 |         code,
  214 |         action,
  215 |         message: defaultMessageFor(code, attempt.verb),
  216 |         domain: attempt.domain,
  217 |       },
  218 |     };
  219 |   }
  220 | 
  221 |   if (attempt.domain === "exegesis" && ctx.exegesisThreadReadCapReached) {
  222 |     const code: GateCode = "EXEGESIS_THREAD_READ_CAP_REACHED";
  223 |     const action = defaultActionFor(code);
  224 |     return {
  225 |       ok: false,
  226 |       uiMode: defaultUiModeFor(attempt.domain, code, attempt.verb, intent),
  227 |       cta: { action, label: "Sign in" },
  228 |       reason: {
  229 |         code,
  230 |         action,
  231 |         message: defaultMessageFor(code, attempt.verb),
  232 |         domain: attempt.domain,
  233 |       },
  234 |     };
  235 |   }
  236 | 
  237 |   if (ctx.readReceiptsCapReached) {
  238 |     const code: GateCode = "READ_RECEIPTS_CAP_REACHED";
  239 |     const action = defaultActionFor(code);
  240 |     return {
  241 |       ok: false,
  242 |       uiMode: defaultUiModeFor(attempt.domain, code, attempt.verb, intent),
  243 |       cta: { action, label: "Unlock identity" },
  244 |       reason: {
  245 |         code,
  246 |         action,
  247 |         message: defaultMessageFor(code, attempt.verb),
  248 |         domain: attempt.domain,
  249 |       },
  250 |     };
  251 |   }
  252 | 
  253 |   // 3) Auth gate (only for verbs that are not allowed anonymously).
  254 |   if (!ctx.isSignedIn && !anonAllowedVerb) {
  255 |     const code: GateCode = "AUTH_REQUIRED";
  256 |     const action = defaultActionFor(code);
  257 |     return {
  258 |       ok: false,
  259 |       uiMode: defaultUiModeFor(attempt.domain, code, attempt.verb, intent),
  260 |       cta: { action, label: "Sign in" },
  261 |       reason: {
  262 |         code,
  263 |         action,
  264 |         message: defaultMessageFor(code, attempt.verb),
  265 |         domain: attempt.domain,
  266 |       },
  267 |     };
  268 |   }
  269 | 
  270 |   // 4) Tier / entitlement gates (when provided by adapters).
  271 |   // These should generally be evaluated only when signed-in; otherwise you’ll prefer AUTH_REQUIRED.
  272 |   if (ctx.isSignedIn && ctx.hasTierAccess === false) {
  273 |     const code: GateCode = "TIER_REQUIRED";
  274 |     const action = defaultActionFor(code);
  275 |     return {
  276 |       ok: false,
  277 |       uiMode: defaultUiModeFor(attempt.domain, code, attempt.verb, intent),
  278 |       cta: { action, label: "Upgrade" },
  279 |       reason: {
  280 |         code,
  281 |         action,
  282 |         message: defaultMessageFor(code, attempt.verb),
  283 |         domain: attempt.domain,
  284 |       },
  285 |     };
  286 |   }
  287 | 
  288 |   if (ctx.isSignedIn && ctx.hasEntitlement === false) {
  289 |     const code: GateCode = "ENTITLEMENT_REQUIRED";
  290 |     const action = defaultActionFor(code);
  291 |     return {
  292 |       ok: false,
  293 |       uiMode: defaultUiModeFor(attempt.domain, code, attempt.verb, intent),
  294 |       cta: { action, label: "Become a member" },
  295 |       reason: {
  296 |         code,
  297 |         action,
  298 |         message: defaultMessageFor(code, attempt.verb),
  299 |         domain: attempt.domain,
  300 |       },
  301 |     };
  302 |   }
  303 | 
  304 |   return { ok: true };
  305 | }
  306 | 
  307 | /**
  308 |  * Helper for normalizing server-provided codes when you know the domain.
  309 |  * This is intentionally here (engine layer) so adapters use the same rules.
  310 |  */
  311 | export function normalizeServerGateCode(
  312 |   raw: GateCodeRaw,
  313 |   domain: GateDomain,
  314 | ): GateCodeRaw {
  315 |   return canonicalizeLegacyCapCode(raw, domain);
  316 | }

   49 | /**
   50 |  * Normalize any raw/legacy string into a known code (canonical or legacy).
   51 |  * Unknown strings => null.
   52 |  */
   53 | export function normalizeGateCodeRaw(
   54 |   raw: string | null | undefined,
   55 | ): GateCodeRaw | null {
   56 |   const c = (raw ?? "").trim();
   57 |   if (!c) return null;
   58 | 
   59 |   switch (c) {
   60 |     // canonical
   61 |     case "AUTH_REQUIRED":
   62 |     case "ENTITLEMENT_REQUIRED":
   63 |     case "TIER_REQUIRED":
   64 |     case "EMBARGO":
   65 |     case "PROVISIONING":
   66 |     case "READ_RECEIPTS_CAP_REACHED":
   67 |     case "PLAYBACK_CAP_REACHED":
   68 |     case "JOURNAL_READ_CAP_REACHED":
   69 |     case "EXEGESIS_THREAD_READ_CAP_REACHED":
   70 |     case "INVALID_REQUEST":
   71 |       return c;
   72 | 
   73 |     // legacy
   74 |     case "ANON_CAP_REACHED":
   75 |     case "CAP_REACHED":
   76 |       return c;
   77 | 
   78 |     default:
   79 |       return null;
   80 |   }
   81 | }
   82 | 
   83 | /**
   84 |  * Helper for adapters that only have “raw string” codes.
   85 |  * Unknown strings => null.
   86 |  */
   87 | export function parseGateCodeRaw(
   88 |   raw: string | null | undefined,
   89 | ): GateCodeRaw | null {
   90 |   return normalizeGateCodeRaw(raw);
   91 | }
   92 | 
   93 | /**
   94 |  * One-way canonicalization helper for legacy cap codes.
   95 |  * Use at boundaries when you KNOW the domain context (e.g. playback vs receipts).
   96 |  */
   97 | export function canonicalizeLegacyCapCode(
   98 |   raw: GateCodeRaw,
   99 |   domain: GateDomain,
  100 | ): GateCodeRaw {
  101 |   if (raw !== "ANON_CAP_REACHED" && raw !== "CAP_REACHED") return raw;
  102 | 
  103 |   switch (domain) {
  104 |     case "playback":
  105 |       return "PLAYBACK_CAP_REACHED";
  106 |     case "journal":
  107 |       return "JOURNAL_READ_CAP_REACHED";
  108 |     case "exegesis":
  109 |       return "EXEGESIS_THREAD_READ_CAP_REACHED";
  110 |     default:
  111 |       // No silent semantic drift: if a legacy cap arrives without a known mapping,
  112 |       // keep it legacy and let the adapter/engine handle it explicitly.
  113 |       return raw;
  114 |   }
  115 | }
  116 | 
  117 | /**
  118 |  * Standard server->client blocked payload shape (future-proof).
  119 |  * (Not yet enforced repo-wide; introduced now so endpoints can converge.)
  120 |  */
  121 | export type GatePayload = {

  125 |   message: string;

  129 |    * Optional extra detail for logs / UX copy variants.
  130 |    * Keep as string so the server doesn't leak UI policy.
  131 |    */
  132 |   reason?: string;
  133 | };

   39 | function isObj(v: unknown): v is Record<string, unknown> {
   40 |   return typeof v === "object" && v !== null;
   41 | }
   42 | 
   43 | function safeString(v: unknown): string {
   44 |   return typeof v === "string" ? v : "";
   45 | }
   46 | 
   47 | function normalizeWhitespace(s: string): string {
   48 |   // keep newlines meaningful, but collapse internal runs
   49 |   return s
   50 |     .replace(/\r\n/g, "\n")
   51 |     .replace(/[ \t]+/g, " ")
   52 |     .replace(/\n{3,}/g, "\n\n")
   53 |     .trim();

   56 | function isAllowedUrl(href: string): boolean {
   57 |   const h = href.trim();
   58 |   if (!h) return false;
   59 |   // allow relative, anchors
   60 |   if (h.startsWith("/") || h.startsWith("#")) return true;
   61 | 
   62 |   try {
   63 |     const u = new URL(h);
   64 |     const p = (u.protocol || "").toLowerCase();
   65 |     return p === "http:" || p === "https:" || p === "mailto:";
   66 |   } catch {
   67 |     return false;
   68 |   }
   69 | }
   70 | 
   71 | function sanitizeNode(node: PMNode, errors: string[]): PMNode | null {
   72 |   const type = safeString(node.type);
   73 |   if (!type) return null;
   74 | 
   75 |   if (!ALLOWED_NODE_TYPES.has(type)) {
   76 |     errors.push(`Disallowed node type: ${type}`);
   77 |     return null;
   78 |   }
   79 | 
   80 |   const out: PMNode = { type };
   81 | 
   82 |   // text node
   83 |   if (type === "text") {
   84 |     out.text = safeString(node.text);
   85 |   }
   86 | 
   87 |   type PMMark = {
   88 |     type?: string;
   89 |     attrs?: Record<string, unknown>;
   90 |   };
   91 | 
   92 |   function isPMMark(v: unknown): v is PMMark {
   93 |     return typeof v === "object" && v !== null;
   94 |   }
   95 | 
   96 |   function getMarkType(m: PMMark): string {
   97 |     return safeString(m.type);
   98 |   }
   99 | 
  100 |   function getMarkHref(m: PMMark): string {
  101 |     const attrs = m.attrs;
  102 |     if (!attrs || typeof attrs !== "object") return "";
  103 |     return safeString((attrs as Record<string, unknown>).href);
  104 |   }
  105 | 
  106 |   // marks
  107 |   if (Array.isArray(node.marks) && node.marks.length) {
  108 |     const marksOut: PMNode["marks"] = [];
  109 | 
  110 |     for (const raw of node.marks) {
  111 |       if (!isPMMark(raw)) continue;
  112 | 
  113 |       const mt = getMarkType(raw);
  114 |       if (!mt || !ALLOWED_MARK_TYPES.has(mt)) continue;
  115 | 
  116 |       if (mt === "link") {
  117 |         const href = getMarkHref(raw);
  118 |         if (!isAllowedUrl(href)) continue;
  119 |         marksOut.push({ type: "link", attrs: { href } });
  120 |         continue;
  121 |       }
  122 | 
  123 |       // other marks: keep only the type, drop attrs
  124 |       marksOut.push({ type: mt });
  125 |     }
  126 | 
  127 |     if (marksOut.length) out.marks = marksOut;
  128 |   }
  129 | 
  130 |   // attrs (only if we later allow headings, etc.)
  131 |   // For now: drop attrs except link marks (handled above)
  132 | 
  133 |   // children
  134 |   if (Array.isArray(node.content) && node.content.length) {
  135 |     const next: PMNode[] = [];
  136 |     for (const c of node.content) {
  137 |       if (!c || typeof c !== "object") continue;
  138 |       const sn = sanitizeNode(c as PMNode, errors);
  139 |       if (sn) next.push(sn);
  140 |     }
  141 |     if (next.length) out.content = next;
  142 |   }
  143 | 
  144 |   return out;
  145 | }
  146 | 
  147 | export function validateAndSanitizeTipTapDoc(input: unknown):
  148 |   | {
  149 |       ok: true;
  150 |       doc: TipTapDoc;
  151 |       plain: string;
  152 |     }
  153 |   | {
  154 |       ok: false;
  155 |       error: string;
  156 |     } {
  157 |   // allow null (legacy/plain-only posting)
  158 |   if (input === null || typeof input === "undefined") {
  159 |     return { ok: false, error: "Missing bodyRich." };
  160 |   }
  161 | 
  162 |   // cheap size guard first
  163 |   let raw = "";
  164 |   try {
  165 |     raw = JSON.stringify(input);
  166 |   } catch {
  167 |     return { ok: false, error: "Invalid bodyRich." };
  168 |   }
  169 |   if (raw.length > MAX_JSON_CHARS) {
  170 |     return { ok: false, error: "bodyRich too large." };
  171 |   }
  172 | 
  173 |   if (!isObj(input)) return { ok: false, error: "Invalid bodyRich." };
  174 |   if (input.type !== "doc")
  175 |     return { ok: false, error: "bodyRich must be a doc." };
  176 | 
  177 |   const errors: string[] = [];
  178 |   const root = sanitizeNode(input as PMNode, errors);
  179 | 
  180 |   if (!root || root.type !== "doc") {
  181 |     return { ok: false, error: "Invalid bodyRich doc." };
  182 |   }
  183 | 
  184 |   // derive plain text
  185 |   const plainParts: string[] = [];
  186 |   const walk = (n: PMNode) => {
  187 |     const t = safeString(n.type);
  188 |     if (t === "text") {
  189 |       plainParts.push(safeString(n.text));
  190 |       return;
  191 |     }
  192 |     if (t === "hardBreak") {
  193 |       plainParts.push("\n");
  194 |       return;
  195 |     }
  196 |     if (Array.isArray(n.content)) {
  197 |       for (const c of n.content) walk(c);
  198 |     }
  199 |     // paragraph/list item boundaries become newlines
  200 |     if (t === "paragraph" || t === "listItem" || t === "blockquote") {
  201 |       plainParts.push("\n");
  202 |     }
  203 |   };
  204 |   walk(root);
  205 | 
  206 |   const plain = normalizeWhitespace(plainParts.join(""));
  207 | 
  208 |   if (!plain) return { ok: false, error: "Empty comment." };
  209 |   if (plain.length > MAX_PLAIN_CHARS) {
  210 |     return { ok: false, error: "Comment too long." };
  211 |   }
  212 | 
  213 |   // Optional strictness: reject if we had disallowed types (instead of just stripping)
  214 |   // For now, we strip and continue; if we want strict reject:
  215 |   // if (errors.length) return { ok: false, error: errors[0] };
  216 | 
  217 |   return { ok: true, doc: root as TipTapDoc, plain };
  218 | }

   94 | export type LyricCue = {
   95 |   lineKey: string;
   96 |   tMs: number;
   97 |   text: string;
   98 |   endMs?: number;
   99 | 
  100 |   /**
  101 |    * Optional enrichment: if this cue's lineKey is mapped into a canonical exegesis group.
  102 |    * Present when known; omitted otherwise.
  103 |    */
  104 |   canonicalGroupKey?: string;

  107 | export type TrackLyricsApiOk = {
  108 |   ok: true;
  109 |   recordingId: string;
  110 |   offsetMs: number;
  111 |   cues: LyricCue[];
  112 | 
  113 |   // present in API today, but optional for consumers
  114 |   version?: string;
  115 |   geniusUrl?: string | null;
  116 |   groupMap?: LyricGroupMap;
  117 | 
  118 |   // meta fields the API returns (nullable)
  119 |   trackTitle?: string | null;
  120 |   trackArtist?: string | null;
  121 |   trackCatalogueId?: string | null;
  122 |   albumTitle?: string | null;
  123 |   albumSlug?: string | null;
  124 |   albumCatalogueId?: string | null;
  125 | };
  126 | 
  127 | export function normalizeLyricCues(input: unknown): LyricCue[] {
  128 |   if (!Array.isArray(input)) return [];
  129 |   const out: LyricCue[] = [];
  130 | 
  131 |   for (const c of input) {
  132 |     if (!c || typeof c !== "object") continue;
  133 | 
  134 |     const lineKey = (c as { lineKey?: unknown }).lineKey;
  135 |     const tMs = (c as { tMs?: unknown }).tMs;
  136 |     const text = (c as { text?: unknown }).text;
  137 |     const endMs = (c as { endMs?: unknown }).endMs;
  138 |     const canonicalGroupKey = (c as { canonicalGroupKey?: unknown })
  139 |       .canonicalGroupKey;
  140 | 
  141 |     if (typeof lineKey !== "string" || lineKey.trim().length === 0) continue;
  142 |     if (typeof tMs !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(tMs) || tMs < 0) continue;
  143 |     if (typeof text !== "string" || text.trim().length === 0) continue;
  144 | 
  145 |     const cue: LyricCue = {
  146 |       lineKey: lineKey.trim(),
  147 |       tMs: Math.floor(tMs),
  148 |       text: text.trim(),
  149 |     };
  150 | 
  151 |     if (typeof endMs === "number" && Number.isFinite(endMs) && endMs >= 0) {
  152 |       cue.endMs = Math.floor(endMs);
  153 |     }
  154 | 
  155 |     // optional enrichment
  156 |     if (typeof canonicalGroupKey === "string" && canonicalGroupKey.trim()) {
  157 |       cue.canonicalGroupKey = canonicalGroupKey.trim();
  158 |     }
  159 | 
  160 |     out.push(cue);
  161 |   }
  162 | 
  163 |   out.sort((a, b) => a.tMs - b.tMs);
  164 |   return out;
  165 | }
  166 | 
  167 | export type SanityLyricCue = {
  168 |   _key?: string;
  169 |   tMs?: number;
  170 |   text?: string;
  171 |   endMs?: number;
  172 | };
  173 | 
  174 | // Adapts Sanity cue shape into canonical cue normalization.
  175 | // (Sanity uses `_key` for the line key.)
  176 | export function normalizeLyricCuesFromSanity(
  177 |   input: SanityLyricCue[] | undefined | null,
  178 | ): LyricCue[] {
  179 |   if (!Array.isArray(input)) return [];
  180 |   return normalizeLyricCues(
  181 |     input.map((c) => ({
  182 |       lineKey: c?._key,
  183 |       tMs: c?.tMs,
  184 |       text: c?.text,
  185 |       endMs: c?.endMs,
  186 |       // canonicalGroupKey not present in Sanity lyrics docs
  187 |     })),
  188 |   );
  189 | }
  190 | 
  191 | export function parseTrackLyricsApiOk(raw: unknown): TrackLyricsApiOk | null {
  192 |   if (!raw || typeof raw !== "object") return null;
  193 |   const o = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
  194 |   if (o.ok !== true) return null;
  195 | 
  196 |   if (typeof o.recordingId !== "string" || !o.recordingId.trim()) return null;
  197 |   if (typeof o.offsetMs !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(o.offsetMs))
  198 |     return null;
  199 | 
  200 |   const cues = normalizeLyricCues(o.cues);
  201 | 
  202 |   // groupMap is optional; keep it only if it looks like the right shape
  203 |   const groupMap =
  204 |     o.groupMap && typeof o.groupMap === "object" && !Array.isArray(o.groupMap)
  205 |       ? (o.groupMap as LyricGroupMap)
  206 |       : undefined;
  207 | 
  208 |   const out: TrackLyricsApiOk = {
  209 |     ok: true,
  210 |     recordingId: o.recordingId.trim(),
  211 |     offsetMs: Math.floor(o.offsetMs),
  212 |     cues,
  213 |   };
  214 | 
  215 |   // Optional fields (keep them if present, but don’t over-validate here)
  216 |   if (typeof o.version === "string" && o.version.trim())
  217 |     out.version = o.version.trim();
  218 |   if (typeof o.geniusUrl === "string" || o.geniusUrl === null)
  219 |     out.geniusUrl = o.geniusUrl as string | null;
  220 |   if (groupMap) out.groupMap = groupMap;
  221 | 
  222 |   // Nullable meta fields (type-safe, no `any`)
  223 |   const nullableMetaKeys = [
  224 |     "trackTitle",
  225 |     "trackArtist",
  226 |     "trackCatalogueId",
  227 |     "albumTitle",
  228 |     "albumSlug",
  229 |     "albumCatalogueId",
  230 |   ] as const;
  231 | 
  232 |   type NullableMetaKey = (typeof nullableMetaKeys)[number];
  233 | 
  234 |   for (const k of nullableMetaKeys) {
  235 |     const v = o[k];
  236 |     if (typeof v === "string") {
  237 |       out[k as NullableMetaKey] = v.trim() || null;
  238 |     } else if (v === null) {
  239 |       out[k as NullableMetaKey] = null;
  240 |     }
  241 |   }
  242 | 
  243 |   return out;
  244 | }
